### M26-S04 — Provider webhook — BFF route, internal processing, exact-200 contract (UC-114)

**Agent:** `bff-ts + backend-ts`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-114 (all steps and A1–A6) and UC-134 (main flow), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Provider Webhooks and Internal API (the webhook route and the internal route), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned) (webhook decorator rule) and § Event Handlers, `docs/LOCK_CATALOG.md` § Billing locks (B-R1), `docs/TESTING_STRATEGY.md` § Testing the billing provider — fake, sandbox, contract suite and clock, `docs/BFF_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Module & Controller Naming Conventions, `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md` (guards vs interceptors; `@Public()`), `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: Subscription (reactivation), § Aggregate: Charge, `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.billing_accounts` (the UNIQUE standalone index on `(provider, provider_customer_id)`) and § `billing.subscriptions` (`current_period_*`, `next_due_date`, `anchor_day`)
**Dependencies:** M26-S01 (webhook token, `FEATURE_FLAG_BILLING_PROVIDER_LIVE`), M26-S02 (charge model, inbox, factory), M26-S03 (events and topics), M25-S14 (provider port, `BillingProviderRegistry` and fake), M25-S15b (suspend / reactivate events and cache invalidation), M25-S13 (access cache invalidation), M25-S12 (`IClock`), M25-S08 (aggregates and repositories), M25-S16 / M25-S20 / M25-S21b (the gating decorators every new route carries)
**Pattern:** Adapter (provider webhook → normalised `ProviderEvent`) + **one shared application service `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`** (M26-D1); Transaction Script with a subscription row lock (B-R1).

**Description:**
Make the provider's word the source of truth, safely.

**BFF** (`billing.webhook.public.controller.ts`, in the existing `features/billing` module but its own controller per the BFF naming rule — it is public and provider-called, never mixed with the authenticated `billing.controller.ts`): `POST /v1/public/billing/webhooks/asaas` with `@Public()` **and** `@BypassWebOnlyGuard()` (the provider sends no web key) **and** `@SkipThrottle()` **and** `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(ANY_AUTHENTICATED)` (no tenant slug arrives, so the access guard passes it untouched; the annotations satisfy the enforcing route-completeness detector, M25-S16/S24), and `@HttpCode(200)` (Nest's default `201` for `POST` would make Asaas count a failure). It verifies the `asaas-access-token` header with a **timing-safe** comparison against `ASAAS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` (wrong / missing → `401`, nothing stored — **authenticity is the BFF's job and its unit test; the backend never sees the token** and `parseWebhook` only normalises), then forwards the already-parsed JSON body to `POST /internal/billing/webhooks/asaas` through a **dedicated tenant-less call** (`BackendHttpService.postWithoutTenant`, new: `BackendHttpService.post()` always sends `X-Tenant-ID: ''` for a caller without a user). The body is not kept as raw bytes: Asaas authenticates with a static token header, not a body signature, and the BFF's JSON parser re-serialises the object (raw-body preservation is Stripe's need, M28, and is not built here). While `FEATURE_FLAG_BILLING_PROVIDER_LIVE` is `false` (production before S39) the route answers exactly `200` as a logged no-op after the token check and calls no backend.

**Backend** (`InternalApiGuard`, `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(ANY_AUTHENTICATED)`): `ProcessProviderWebhookUseCase`. **Provider resolution** (every M26 use case obtains the adapter from `BillingProviderRegistry.resolve(merchantProfileKey)`, never a bare port): before the tenant is known the adapter is resolved from the provider in the route plus `resolveMerchantProfileKey(APP_ENV, 'BR')` (M25-S07) and used only for `parseWebhook` (local, no network); once the tenant is known, `fetchChargeStatus` uses the registry entry of the **subscription's own** `merchant_profile_key`. The use case resolves the tenant from the provider customer id and, when that customer is not yet known locally, from the payment's `externalReference` (our charge id, which exists locally as a `PENDING` charge before the provider is ever called; the field's presence in the webhook payload was observed in the sandbox (`docs/ASAAS_PROVIDER.md` § 2, check 11) and is re-checked in S37) so a webhook that outruns S05's customer save is not dropped (neither resolves → `200`, logged, no state change — A2), normalises the event (`PaymentConfirmed` / `PaymentOverdue` / `PaymentRefunded` / `PaymentChargeback`; auto-billing events are not handled in M26) and, in **one transaction**: `ProviderEvent.insertIfAbsent` (duplicate → `200`, no-op), then `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` — which loads the subscription `findByTenantIdForUpdate` (B-R1) **first**, then the charge, applies the fact and writes the outbox rows (`ChargePaid`, `SubscriptionReactivated`, `SubscriptionSuspended` as applicable). **Any event the adapter does not normalise** (Asaas also sends `PAYMENT_CREATED`, `PAYMENT_UPDATED`, `PAYMENT_DELETED` and more), an unknown customer **and** unknown `externalReference`, and an amount mismatch after the re-read all end in **exactly `200`**, logged, with no state change — a non-2xx would count toward Asaas's documented pause rule (15 consecutive failures, `docs/ASAAS_PROVIDER.md` G3) for events that are not failures.

**The paid signal is method-dependent**: Asaas Pix emits only `PAYMENT_RECEIVED`; boleto `CONFIRMED` then `RECEIVED`; card `CONFIRMED` then `RECEIVED` ~32 days later — the first of `CONFIRMED` / `RECEIVED` for a charge yields the single `PaymentConfirmed`, the later is a no-op. **For Asaas a state-changing event is applied only after `fetchChargeStatus` confirms it and the amount matches** (a leaked token cannot forge a payment) — that re-read is a provider call and therefore happens **before** the transaction opens, never inside it; it also supplies the `receiptUrl` (`NormalizedChargeStatus.receiptUrl`, M25-S14), stored by `markPaid(method, paidAt, receiptUrl | null, now)` and carried by `ChargePaid`. To protect the provider's rate limit (25,000 requests per 12 h per account, `docs/ASAAS_PROVIDER.md` A13) a replayed or duplicate delivery must not cost a re-read: a cheap read-only `ProviderEvent` existence check by `(provider, eventId)` runs first and returns `200` with no provider call when the event is already in the inbox (the authoritative dedupe stays `insertIfAbsent` inside the transaction).

**What a paid fact writes (one definition; the aggregate methods are unit-tested per row, and S06/S20 reuse them):**
- *PERIOD charge, subscription `TRIALING`:* the charge is marked paid and `has_ever_paid` is set; the subscription stays `TRIALING` with `current_period_*` untouched (the sweep's `activateAfterTrial`, S06, writes them at trial end).
- *PERIOD charge, subscription `ACTIVE`:* `markPeriodPaid` marks the charge paid, sets `has_ever_paid`, sets `current_period_end = max(current_period_end, charge.period_end)` and sets `current_period_start = charge.period_start` only when that start is today or earlier in the subscription timezone (an invoice issued 10 days early does not change the current period). It never touches `anchor_day` or `next_due_date` (advanced by S05 at issue).
- *PERIOD charge, subscription `PAST_DUE`:* the same writes, and the subscription becomes `ACTIVE` (`past_due_since` and `grace_until` cleared); the period is **not** re-dated — it is the charge's own period.
- *RECOVERY charge, subscription `SUSPENDED` (`NON_PAYMENT`, `PAYMENT_REVERSED`, `CANCELED`):* `reactivateOnPayment` makes the subscription `ACTIVE` (reason, `suspended_at`, the purge-warning stamps and `cancel_at` cleared), sets `current_period_start` = the payment moment, `current_period_end` = one cycle later, `anchor_day` = the payment's tenant-local day clamped to 28, `next_due_date` = the day after the new period's end, and `has_ever_paid`; the recovery charge's own period stays empty. An `OPERATOR` suspension is never lifted by a payment.
- *Stale charge paid:* if the provider could not cancel a stale charge because it was already paid (UC-114 step 4), **that charge is the payment** and is applied by the rule above for its kind, and the recovery charge is cancelled; if both are ever paid, the second is recorded `REFUNDED` (the actual refund is made in the provider console).
- *Refund / chargeback:* a partial refund (`refunded_amount_minor` only) and an opened dispute change no access — an opened dispute is only logged with a structured `billing_dispute_opened` field (S36 turns it into a metric and an alert; no new table). A **full confirmed refund** or a **completed chargeback** (the chargeback leg only for an event verified as finalizing a lost dispute — `docs/ASAAS_PROVIDER.md` A11, G5, S37) that leaves the current period without settled payment suspends immediately with `PAYMENT_REVERSED` (`ApplyChargeFactUseCase` inserts the `RECOVERY` charge `PENDING` through the S02 factory in the same transaction; creating it at the provider after commit is the `CreateRecoveryChargeAtProviderUseCase` that S06 introduces and adds to this use case's after-commit step — until S06 ships, S05's retry creates it). **Exception — a full refund of the first invoice while the subscription is `TRIALING`:** nothing settled is being reversed, so there is no `PAYMENT_REVERSED`; the charge becomes `REFUNDED` and, because `UQ_billing_charges_period` ignores only `CANCELED` charges (a replacement period charge is impossible), S06's trial-end rule treats a `REFUNDED` first charge as unpaid and the recovery path applies at trial end. (This differs from the "re-issue" wording of the decision record; the canonical docs `DOMAIN_MODEL`, `RUNBOOKS` and UC-113 are aligned to this in the audit fix.)
- *`PaymentOverdue` is confirm-only:* after the re-read it records the inbox row and a log line and changes no charge or subscription state — the local due-date sweep (S06 rule 1) alone owns `PENDING` → `OVERDUE` and `PAST_DUE`, and a webhook never sets `past_due_since`.
- *Out-of-order:* "legal" is defined in S02 (a provider-verified paid fact may revive a `CANCELED` charge; nothing pays a `REFUNDED` / `CHARGEBACK` one); an illegal event is logged and left to reconcile (S07).

After commit: invalidate the access cache. Response: **exactly `200`** after the commit — never `201`, `204` or a redirect; a backend failure returns non-2xx so the provider retries.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`ProcessProviderWebhookUseCase`**: parse and tenant resolution, normalisation, the `fetchChargeStatus` re-read outside the transaction, then the single transaction above.
2. **`ApplyChargeFactUseCase`** (shared, M26-D1): the paid / refunded / chargeback transitions on a charge and the resulting subscription transition (an overdue fact is confirm-only); called by S07 and S15 later.
**Backend HTTP surface:** new controller `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-billing-webhook.controller.ts` — `POST /internal/billing/webhooks/{provider}` (`InternalApiGuard`, `@NoFeatureGate()`, `@SuspensionAccess(ANY_AUTHENTICATED)`).
**BFF endpoint spec:** `POST /v1/public/billing/webhooks/asaas` — no JWT, no tenant header; request = the provider's JSON; response = empty `200`. (`/stripe` is M28.)
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none new (env and `FEATURE_FLAG_BILLING_PROVIDER_LIVE` from S01).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/process-provider-webhook.use-case.ts`, `apply-charge-fact.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts` each) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-billing-webhook.controller.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/http/billing/webhooks.http` (new)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.webhook.public.controller.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.component.spec.ts`) (new), `billing.webhook.schemas.ts` (new — the payload is forwarded, not parsed into a DTO)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify — register the webhook controller)
- `apps/bff/src/shared/http/backend-http.service.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — `postWithoutTenant`)
- `apps/bff/http/billing/webhooks.http` (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/subscription.aggregate.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — `markPeriodPaid`, `reactivateOnPayment`, `suspend(PAYMENT_REVERSED)`; M25-S08 deliberately left these to M26; one unit test per row of the "what a paid fact writes" list)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/charge.aggregate.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify only if the paid / refund transitions need an accessor S02 did not define)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A paid Pix, boleto or card charge reactivates a `PAST_DUE` or `SUSPENDED` subscription without any human action, once, regardless of how many times the provider sends the event.
- [ ] A forged request (wrong token) changes nothing; an unknown customer changes nothing; an event type Ikaro does not act on is acknowledged with `200` and changes nothing.
- [ ] A full refund that leaves the period unpaid suspends the tenant (`PAYMENT_REVERSED`); a lost chargeback does the same **once the Asaas event that finalizes a lost dispute is verified** (`docs/ASAAS_PROVIDER.md` A11 / G5, S37) — until then `CHARGEBACK_LOST` is proven on the fake only and the Asaas normalizer maps an unverified chargeback event to "no state change, log"; a partial refund or an opened dispute never changes access; a full refund of the first invoice during the trial never suspends by itself.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] BFF: wrong / missing token → `401`; timing-safe comparison used; success → exactly `200` (not `201`); flag off → `200` no-op and no backend call; the tenant-less call sends no `X-Tenant-ID`
  - [ ] Normalisation table for the three payment methods (Pix `RECEIVED` only; boleto `CONFIRMED`→`RECEIVED`; card `CONFIRMED`→`RECEIVED`), including "whichever arrives first wins, the later is a no-op"
  - [ ] `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`: each row of the "what a paid fact writes" list (period fields, `anchor_day`, `next_due_date` per charge kind and status); the already-paid-stale-charge case; the double-paid refund case; out-of-order paid-after-cancelled applied only when provider-verified
  - [ ] Negative guarantees, each with its own test: a **partial refund** changes no access; an **opened dispute** changes no access and only logs `billing_dispute_opened`; `PaymentOverdue` changes no charge or subscription state and never sets `past_due_since`; a full refund of the first invoice while `TRIALING` produces `REFUNDED` and no suspension
  - [ ] The `fetchChargeStatus` mismatch (amount differs) → event not applied, logged, `200`; an unnormalised event type → `200`, nothing stored
  - [ ] A duplicate delivery already in the inbox returns `200` with **no** `fetchChargeStatus` call (the pre-check)
  - [ ] `markPaid` receives the `receiptUrl` from the re-read and `ChargePaid` carries it
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end on the fake: simulate each delivery option from the fake — NORMAL, DUPLICATE, DELAYED, OUT_OF_ORDER, provider 5xx — and assert the resulting charge / subscription state each time
  - [ ] **Concurrency (B-R1):** two webhooks for one charge arriving together produce one `ChargePaid`; a webhook racing a `suspend` on the same subscription serialises (the row lock holds, no lost update)
  - [ ] Inbox idempotency: replaying the same `eventId` is a `200` no-op with no second outbox row
  - [ ] The transaction contains no provider I/O (`transactional-io` detector passes; the re-read happens before)
  - [ ] A webhook that arrives before the customer id was stored, or before the charge is `READY`, is resolved through `externalReference` and applied
  - [ ] Unknown customer **and** unknown `externalReference` → `200`; missing `InternalApiGuard` key → `401`
  - [ ] Both controllers carry `@NoFeatureGate()` and an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)` (the route-completeness detector passes)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] a webhook resolves exactly one tenant from the customer id and never touches another tenant's charges; two tenants with the same amount and due date are not confused
- E2E: none — covered by integration (the full money loop E2E is S35)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S05 — Issue the next charge, creation retry and the `cron-billing-issue-charges` trigger (UC-113)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-113 (all of it, A1–A11), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.subscriptions` (indexes) and § `billing.charges`, `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: Charge (price snapshot), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing cron triggers, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned) (trigger jobs; Billing's jobs do not use the shared tenant-job helper) and § Transactions, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_TESTING.md` § Standalone index for a cross-tenant system job, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` § Billing configuration (planned)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charges, factory, the creation lease), M26-S03 (`ChargeIssued` topic), M26-S04 (`ApplyChargeFactUseCase`: a webhook that lands between the insert and `READY` is applied by it), M25-S04 (`GetFeaturePriceAtUseCase`), M25-S14 (provider port, `BillingProviderRegistry` + fake), M25-S12 (`IClock`), M25-S13 (the tenant-info port `BillingPlatformPort`, which this story extends with `isActive`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 / M25-S21b (gating decorators on the new controller)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → job use case selecting through a standalone index, one subscription per transaction **taking the subscription row lock `findByTenantIdForUpdate` (B-R1) first** — it inserts a charge and advances `next_due_date`, a subscription write that must serialise with a plan save (S19) and a webhook (S04); **PENDING → READY** two-step creation (M26-D3); its own standalone-index selection with no operational-status filter (the retry query reaches `SUSPENDED` tenants' recovery charges).

**Description:**
Issue every due charge exactly once. A cron publisher `CronBillingController` (`POST /cron/billing-issue-charges`, `InternalApiGuard`, local / manual only, `@NoFeatureGate()` + an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(ANY_AUTHENTICATED)`) publishes the trigger `cron-billing-issue-charges` (`CRON_BILLING_ISSUE_CHARGES_TRIGGER`); `IssueNextChargesTriggerHandler` (class name unique across the codebase) is registered with `ITriggerBus.registerTrigger` and calls `IssueNextChargesUseCase`. **Selection:** subscriptions `TRIALING` or `ACTIVE`, not `exempt`, not cancelling beyond the period, whose `next_due_date` is within `CHARGE_ISSUE_LEAD_DAYS` (10), through the standalone partial index on `next_due_date` created in M25-S03 (`EXPLAIN` asserted) via a `findDueForIssueForJob` selector added to `ISubscriptionRepository` (the M25-S08 repository exposes only `findByTenantId`, `findByTenantIdForUpdate` and `save`). The query selects on the UTC date plus the lead plus one day of slack, then each subscription re-checks `next_due_date <= today(subscription timezone) + CHARGE_ISSUE_LEAD_DAYS` before it is processed (tenant-local dates, as in S06). **Per subscription, one transaction:** compute the lines from the items scheduled for the next service period, each amount read from the price version in force on that period's **start date** (`effective_from <= period_start`); insert the `Charge` (`PERIOD`, `providerCreationStatus = PENDING`) and its lines, and advance `next_due_date` by one cycle in the **same** transaction (`UNIQUE(tenant_id, subscription_id, period_start)` makes a re-run a no-op). Then, **outside** the transaction (`transactional-io` passes), the charge is **claimed** with S02's `claimForProviderCreation` lease (a creator that does not win the claim stops; the lease is why the manual `POST /cron/…`, a second instance and the retry query below can never create two provider charges), the adapter comes from `BillingProviderRegistry.resolve(subscription.merchantProfileKey)`, and the provider is asked for an existing charge with our charge id as `externalReference` before one is created; then `ensureCustomer` (syncing the identity, BR tax id required, with our tenant/customer reference as `externalReference` so a repeated call updates the same provider customer; the returned `provider_customer_id` is saved on the `BillingAccount` in its own short transaction **before** `createCharge`, so a fast webhook can resolve the tenant) and `createCharge` (`billingType = UNDEFINED`, our charge id as `externalReference`, and the `returnUrl` of the web billing page — a new input of the port's `createCharge`, M26-D10, used by S28 and mapped to Asaas's callback in S37 where allowed); then a further short transaction stores the provider id, payment link, expiry and `READY` (if a webhook already marked the charge paid in between, this step stores the ids but leaves the status alone and `ChargeIssued` is not published for a paid charge); **`ChargeIssued` is published only after `READY`**. **Creation retry** runs inside the same job: any charge left `PENDING` is re-attempted — through the same claim and `externalReference` lookup — without inserting a duplicate period charge (A2); a charge held for lack of identity (below) makes no provider call and does not increment `provider_creation_attempts`, so it never trips the "creation stuck" alert (S36: a charge `PENDING` provider creation with 3 or more attempts or older than 1 hour). **TRIALING:** the first charge is issued from the manager's **confirmed** selection (`plan_confirmed_at` set — S19), due at trial end, as soon as it is within the 10-day lead; if still unconfirmed at `trial_ends_at − PLAN_CONFIRM_FALLBACK_DAYS` (3) the job issues it from the default paid items and the email states which features and total were invoiced (A10). **Holds:** BR customer without a valid CPF / CNPJ (A8) or without the full billing address (A9) → no provider charge and **no charge row either** (the subscription is simply left due, so nothing is inserted and `next_due_date` does not advance), a banner flag on the summary (`summary.addressRequired`, banner copy points to "Dados de cobrança") — no extra email kind is invented; the trial-ending reminders (S09) already warn before the end; issuing **resumes automatically** when the identity is saved (S16 triggers a re-evaluation by leaving the subscription due) on the next run; no operator step. **The one other hold is the sweep-created `RECOVERY` charge** (S06 at a `NON_PAYMENT` / `PAYMENT_REVERSED` suspension — nobody is at the keyboard): it is inserted `PENDING` as usual but the provider customer and charge are not created while the CPF / CNPJ or the full billing address is missing; the charge stays `PENDING` with no payment link, `summary.addressRequired` is true, the suspended manager (who can still reach the billing page) is pointed to "Dados de cobrança" by the banner, the suspended page and the S12 suspended email (which says the payment link follows in the next email), and `POST …/payment-link` on it answers `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`; saving the identity lets this story's retry query create it and `ChargeIssued` then sends the link. **A manager's own reactivate (UC-134, S20) never creates a held charge**: with no address it answers `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` and creates nothing (UC-134 A6 stands). This sweep-hold extension is documented in UC-115 by the docs owner. A **trial held for lack of an address** keeps its end date — the hold never extends access: S06 rule 2 suspends it if no charge is paid, and its recovery charge is held the same way until the address is saved. **Skips:** `exempt` (A1), `SUSPENDED` (A4), `cancelAt` passed for the period (A5), `is_active = false` (A11 — the M27 reference; guard written now, read through the M25 tenant-info port `BillingPlatformPort`, which **this story** extends with an `isActive` field by extending Platform's `get-tenant-billing-info.use-case.ts` (its output type and spec), backed by the existing `platform.tenants.is_active` column; S08 later adds the list method to the same port — the access answer does not carry it, so no second port is created), no renewable price version (A6 — never issued; a logged alert only). **A6 is prevented by a CI / seed-consistency test, never by a boot crash** (a bad price row must not take the whole backend down): the test asserts that every country with prices in the seed migration has a complete set — a `selectable` or in-use price for every (feature, tier) the country's plan can hold — and that every country the app can sign up (BR only until M28; the US list is seeded everywhere, see UC-127 A7) is among them; at runtime a missing renewable price skips that subscription, logs `billing_price_missing` with the subscription id and lets the job continue.

**Devops half (cron trigger).** New trigger → playbook row "A new Pub/Sub topic (… cron trigger) + its app code". **PR sequence:** 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply. PR1 (`infra-app-mix-ok`): the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated (the topic auto-provisions from it — no `envs/*` pubsub edit) **and the `google_cloud_scheduler_job` entry (`ikaro-cron-billing-issue-charges`) in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` in this same PR** (`variables.tf` rejects any catalog `cron-*` topic with no matching job); schedule daily, **created paused in production**. The scheduler module has no pause switch today, so this story adds it: a module variable `paused_job_keys` (set of `locals.jobs` keys, default empty) feeding `paused = contains(var.paused_job_keys, each.key)` on `google_cloud_scheduler_job.cron`, set in `envs/prod/main.tf` to the six billing job keys (S39 empties it after launch readiness) and left empty in staging. The module's hard-coded test fixture (`tests/resources_unit_test.tftest.hcl` lists the cron topic map and asserts `length(...) == 8`) and the "all 8" text of `cron_topic_ids` in `variables.tf` are updated here, and **every later trigger story (S06–S10) updates the same fixture and count** for its own job; then the manual Foundation apply.
**IAM / permissions:** scheduler publisher → the new trigger topic; push-subscription invoker for the backend — both derived from the catalog by Foundation; no new principal.
**Live-verification check:** on staging after the Foundation apply: `gcloud pubsub topics get-iam-policy projects/<staging>/topics/ikaro-cron-billing-issue-charges` populated; then `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-issue-charges --location=<region>` and observe the handler log line with its `correlationId` and the count of subscriptions processed (zero is a valid pass on an empty staging). Pass = IAM policy populated and one handler log observed.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`IssueNextChargesUseCase`** (the job) and its per-subscription **`IssueChargeForSubscriptionUseCase`** (two transactions as above), 2. **`RetryPendingChargeCreationUseCase`** (the retry query).
**Backend HTTP surface:** new controller `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-issue-charges` (publisher only; `InternalApiGuard`; `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(ANY_AUTHENTICATED)`).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A subscription ten days from its due date gets exactly one charge with a payment link and one email; running the job twice, or crashing between steps, never creates a second charge or loses the first.
- [ ] A trial that has confirmed its plan is invoiced from the confirmed selection; one that has not is invoiced from the default selection three days before the end, and the email says plainly what was invoiced.
- [ ] A tenant without a valid CPF / CNPJ or billing address gets no provider charge, sees the banner pointing to "Dados de cobrança", and is invoiced automatically on the next run once the identity is saved.
- [ ] Exempt, suspended, cancelling-past-the-period and deactivated tenants are never invoiced.
- [ ] A sweep-created recovery charge for a tenant without a valid CPF / CNPJ or billing address is held and becomes payable (and emailed) as soon as the identity is saved; a trial held for lack of an address is still suspended on its end date.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Line computation reads each price from the version in force at the **period start** (a version effective after the period start is ignored; an invoice issued early uses its future period's price)
  - [ ] Each alternative flow A1–A11 (one named test each), the trial first-charge rule and the fallback day
  - [ ] Handler calls exactly one use case, rethrows, passes `correlationId`
- Integration:
  - [ ] **Concurrency (B-R1):** the issue job racing a plan save (S19) or a webhook on one subscription serialises — exactly one charge for the period carrying the final selection (S19 owns the named race test; this story provides the lock)
  - [ ] **Concurrency (creation lease):** two concurrent creators (the issue job twice, and the issue job racing the retry query) produce exactly one provider charge on the fake, and the second finds the first through the `externalReference` lookup
  - [ ] The provider calls (`ensureCustomer`, `createCharge`) are outside any transaction (`transactional-io` passes)
  - [ ] **Idempotency:** running the job twice produces one charge; killing the process after the first transaction and re-running resumes at the provider call without a duplicate (`PENDING` → `READY`)
  - [ ] Provider failure (fake 5xx) leaves the charge `PENDING`; the retry query re-attempts it without a duplicate period charge; `ChargeIssued` is not published before `READY`
  - [ ] `provider_customer_id` is committed before `createCharge` is called; a webhook that lands before `READY` is applied (S04 resolves it) and the `READY` step keeps the paid status and publishes no `ChargeIssued`
  - [ ] The job's `EXPLAIN` uses the `next_due_date` partial index; one slow subscription does not block the rest (per-run limit + per-subscription transaction)
  - [ ] The price-set consistency spec fails when a seeded country has an incomplete price set; at runtime a missing renewable price skips that subscription with `billing_price_missing` and the job continues (no boot failure)
  - [ ] Hold: a held `PERIOD` issue inserts nothing and leaves `next_due_date`; a sweep-created `RECOVERY` charge held for a missing address makes no provider call and increments no attempts
  - [ ] A subscription whose tenant-local date is one day ahead of or behind UTC is selected exactly when its local `next_due_date` is within the lead (the ±1 day slack plus the local re-check)
  - [ ] The controller carries `@NoFeatureGate()` and an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)` (route-completeness detector)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] one run issues charges for several tenants, each charge carrying its own `tenantId`; the job never reads `RequestContext`
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] Checkov / Tfsec clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S06 — Status sweep and the `cron-billing-status-sweep` trigger (UC-115)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-115 (all of it), UC-134 (recovery charge), `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: Subscription (status ladder, settlement margins, `graceUntil`), § Context 7 (constants), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.subscriptions` (partial indexes on `trial_ends_at`, `past_due_since`) and § `billing.provider_health`, `docs/LOCK_CATALOG.md` § Billing locks (B-R1; the open-recovery unique), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned) and § Transactions, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charges, recovery factory), M26-S03 (`SubscriptionPastDue` topic and the events), M26-S05 (the trigger constants file and `CronBillingController` this story extends), M25-S15b (`SubscriptionSuspended` events), M25-S13 (cache invalidation), M25-S14 (`BillingProviderRegistry`, the typed `ChargeAlreadyPaidError`), M25-S12 (`IClock`), M26-S04 (`ApplyChargeFactUseCase` for the stale-charge-already-paid case and the trial-end re-read, and `suspend(PAYMENT_REVERSED)`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 / M25-S21b (gating decorators on the controller)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → sweep use case, **one transaction per subscription** with the subscription row lock (B-R1); the recovery-charge factory from S02; its own standalone-index selection with no operational-status filter (the sweep must also evaluate `SUSPENDED` / non-operational tenants — cancel-at, recovery charge, freeze). Provider I/O (the trial-end re-read, the stale-charge cancel, the recovery-charge creation) is **always outside the transaction** (M26-D3).

**Description:**
Make time move the ladder. Hourly trigger `cron-billing-status-sweep` (`CRON_BILLING_STATUS_SWEEP_TRIGGER`; publisher `POST /cron/billing-status-sweep` on the existing `CronBillingController`, which keeps `@NoFeatureGate()` + an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(ANY_AUTHENTICATED)` on the new route); `StatusSweepTriggerHandler` calls `SweepSubscriptionStatusesUseCase`. All dates are evaluated in `subscriptions.timezone` against `IClock.now()`; `exempt` subscriptions are never touched; each rule selects through its own standalone index via a `…ForJob` selector added to `ISubscriptionRepository` (and, for rule 1, to the charge repository), on the UTC date with one day of slack and a per-subscription local-date re-check, exactly as S05. Rules, each a separate private step with its own tests: **(1) Overdue** — a `PENDING` charge past its tenant-local due date becomes `OVERDUE` (this sweep is the only writer of `OVERDUE` and `past_due_since`; S04 treats the webhook's `PaymentOverdue` as confirm-only); for an `ACTIVE` subscription it becomes `PAST_DUE` with `past_due_since` (event `SubscriptionPastDue`). **(2) Trial end** — a `TRIALING` subscription whose `trialEndsAt` plus the settlement margin has passed (`PAYMENT_SETTLEMENT_MARGIN_HOURS` 24 h, or `PAYMENT_PENDING_MARGIN_BUSINESS_DAYS` 3 business days — Monday to Friday in the tenant calendar, **no holiday calendar in M26** (a known limitation, stated) — when `payment_return_seen_at` shows the manager returned while the charge is still open) is re-read from the provider **before the per-subscription transaction opens** (never inside it) — **if the provider says the first charge is paid, the fact is routed through `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` (S04) and the tenant is not suspended** (a missed webhook at trial end must never lock out a paying tenant; rule 5 then activates it); with its first charge unpaid (a `REFUNDED` first charge counts as unpaid, S04), or no charge at all, it becomes `SUSPENDED` (`NON_PAYMENT`) **directly** — there is no `PAST_DUE` after a trial; access is unchanged during the margin. **(3) Past due** — a `PAST_DUE` subscription becomes `SUSPENDED` (`NON_PAYMENT`) when its grace has run out: if `grace_until` is set, when `now > grace_until` (the operator override replaces the 14 days; M25-S15b's `ExtendGrace` refuses a date earlier than `past_due_since + 14 days`), otherwise when `now > past_due_since + 14 days`. **On every `NON_PAYMENT` suspension the sweep cancels the stale open charge (locally and at the provider) and issues the recovery charge** (a `PAYMENT_REVERSED` suspension is applied by the webhook, S04, not by the sweep; `CANCELED` and `OPERATOR` issue none) through the S02 factory, whose price map this use case resolves with `GetFeaturePriceAtUseCase` for the subscription-timezone date: the stale charge is cancelled locally and flagged `provider_cancel_pending` and the `RECOVERY` charge is inserted `PENDING` in the suspension's transaction; **after commit** the provider side runs through two shared use cases **owned by this story** — `CancelOpenChargesUseCase` (local `CANCELED` + `provider_cancel_pending`, then the provider cancel, clearing the flag on success; S19, S20 and M25-S15b's `set-tenant-exempt` hook depend on it) and `CreateRecoveryChargeAtProviderUseCase` (the PENDING → READY recipe, M26-D3, behind S02's creation lease and the `externalReference` lookup; S20 reuses it, and this story adds its call to S04's `ProcessProviderWebhookUseCase` after-commit step for a `PAYMENT_REVERSED` recovery charge) — so the manager lands on a payable "Pague 1 mês para reativar" (UC-115) within the same sweep run, and S05's retry query picks up any left `PENDING`. If the provider refuses to cancel because the stale charge is already paid (the port's typed `ChargeAlreadyPaidError`), that charge is the payment and the case follows `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` (S04) instead (A3); any other cancel failure leaves `provider_cancel_pending` set for reconcile (S07). If the billing identity or address is incomplete, the recovery charge is held `PENDING` without a provider call (S05 holds — the sweep-created recovery charge is the one hold in M26): the suspension still happens, `summary.addressRequired` is set, and the S12 suspended email points to "Dados de cobrança" and says the payment link follows. **(4) Cancel-at** — `cancelAt` reached → `SUSPENDED` (`CANCELED`) (no recovery charge for `CANCELED`). **(5) Trial → paid** — a `TRIALING` subscription past `trialEndsAt` whose first charge is `PAID` becomes `ACTIVE` through `activateAfterTrial`: `current_period_start` = `trialEndsAt`, `current_period_end` = one cycle later (the first charge's own period), `anchor_day` = the trial-end local day clamped to 28; `next_due_date` is left as S05 advanced it. **(6)** A confirmed payment converts `PAST_DUE` / `SUSPENDED` to `ACTIVE` through S04, never here. **Suspension freeze (A4):** `billing.provider_health` has one row per `merchant_profile_key`; its aggregate, repository and entity are created in this story (S07 and S08 reuse them) and the sweep reads the row **of each subscription's own `merchant_profile_key`**; **no row means not frozen** (the row is created lazily by the first reconcile / health write, tested). When that row's `freeze_active` is set (written by S08; `false` until S08 ships) the sweep still marks `OVERDUE` / `PAST_DUE` but suspends nobody **for non-payment** (rules 2 and 3). `cancelAt` suspensions (rule 4) are **exempt from the freeze**: they are the manager's own decision at a known date, not a payment judgement, so a provider outage must not extend a cancelled subscription. The provider cancel call is **outside** the transaction; if it fails the local state still changes, `provider_cancel_pending` stays set and reconcile (S07) retries the provider side. A payment arriving during the sweep: the row lock makes the paid state win (A2). Each suspension publishes `SubscriptionSuspended` and invalidates the access cache after commit.

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, the regenerated catalog and the `ikaro-cron-billing-status-sweep` `locals.jobs` scheduler entry in the same PR, plus the scheduler test fixture and job count; no `envs/*` edit — S05 already put the key in `paused_job_keys`; then the manual Foundation apply); schedule **hourly**, created paused in production (S39 enables it). **IAM / permissions:** derived from the catalog; no new principal. **Live-verification check:** `gcloud pubsub topics get-iam-policy …` populated; `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-status-sweep` and observe the handler log with its processed counts. Pass = both observed.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`SweepSubscriptionStatusesUseCase`** (the job; one `SweepOneSubscriptionUseCase` per candidate subscription, each in its own transaction with `findByTenantIdForUpdate`).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-status-sweep` (publisher only).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] An unpaid trial is suspended one day after it ends (three business days when the manager already returned from a boleto page), with no `PAST_DUE` in between; an unpaid `ACTIVE` charge becomes `PAST_DUE` the day after its due date and `SUSPENDED` fourteen days later with a ready-to-pay recovery charge.
- [ ] A payment that lands during the sweep always wins.
- [ ] While the provider is unhealthy (freeze), nobody is newly suspended for non-payment (a tenant's own scheduled cancel still takes effect).

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Every rule 1–5 with table-driven dates, including a UTC-3 tenant and a DST zone, the 24 h and 3-business-day (Monday–Friday) margins, and `grace_until` overriding the 14 days in both directions (a later `grace_until` extends, an earlier one never shortens)
  - [ ] `exempt` never touched; running twice is idempotent (A1)
  - [ ] Recovery charge issued on `NON_PAYMENT` (the `PAYMENT_REVERSED` one belongs to S04), not on `CANCELED` or `OPERATOR`; the factory receives the price map for the subscription-timezone date
- Integration:
  - [ ] **Concurrency (B-R1):** the sweep deciding to suspend while a webhook confirms payment on the same subscription serialises and the paid state wins; the loser leaves no recovery charge
  - [ ] **Concurrency (`UQ_billing_charges_open_recovery`):** two sweeps overlapping on one subscription produce one open recovery charge
  - [ ] Provider cancel failure → the local state still changes, `provider_cancel_pending` stays set and the suspension is not rolled back; the `ChargeAlreadyPaidError` refusal follows the paid path
  - [ ] **Trial-end re-read:** a `TRIALING` subscription past `trialEndsAt + margin` whose first charge the provider reports `PAID` (webhook missed) is not suspended — the fact is applied and rule 5 activates it; the same with the provider reporting it unpaid suspends
  - [ ] **Concurrency (creation lease):** a suspension's post-commit recovery creation racing S05's retry produces one provider charge
  - [ ] No row in `provider_health` for the profile key means not frozen
  - [ ] No provider I/O inside any sweep transaction (`transactional-io` passes): the trial-end re-read happens before it, the stale-charge cancel and the recovery-charge creation after commit; a suspension leaves a `READY` recovery charge with a payment link within the same run (fake provider)
  - [ ] Freeze active → `PAST_DUE` still recorded, no non-payment suspension; the freeze row is looked up by the subscription's `merchant_profile_key` (a frozen profile does not freeze another); a `cancelAt` suspension still happens under a freeze
  - [ ] A suspension for a tenant with an incomplete billing address leaves a `PENDING` recovery charge with no provider call, and S05's retry creates it once the address is saved
  - [ ] Each rule's selection uses its standalone index (`EXPLAIN`); one failing subscription does not stop the rest
  - [ ] The controller route carries `@NoFeatureGate()` and an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)`
- Tenant isolation: [ ] each transition touches only its own tenant's rows; the job passes explicit `tenantId`s
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above

### M26-S07 — Reconcile, the on-demand refresh and the `cron-billing-reconcile` trigger (UC-116)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-116 (all of it), UC-114 (step 3: the shared apply), UC-119 (the on-demand refresh), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.provider_health`, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing cron triggers, `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` § Billing (planned) (Drift, Provider incident), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned), `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge selectors), M26-S04 (`ApplyChargeFactUseCase`, the shared apply), M25-S14 (the fake, `BillingProviderRegistry`, `fetchChargeStatus`, `readAccountHealth`), M26-S05 (trigger constants and `CronBillingController`), M26-S06 (the `ProviderHealth` repository and entity; `CancelOpenChargesUseCase` and the `provider_cancel_pending` flag this story's third step retries), M25-S03 (`provider_cancel_pending` column and the `IDX_billing_charges_paid_recent` index), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the cron route)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → job use case (its own standalone-index selection, no operational-status filter); reuses `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` (M26-D1) — no second implementation of the paid transition; a **single-charge refresh use case** for the on-demand path. The provider read (`fetchChargeStatus`) is outside every transaction; only the apply is transactional (`transactional-io` passes).

**Description:**
Guarantee a missed webhook never leaves a paying tenant locked out. Trigger `cron-billing-reconcile` (the doc name is `billing-reconcile`; manual publisher `POST /cron/billing-reconcile`): a **daily** full pass and an **hourly** at-risk pass. **One trigger, one scheduler job, hourly** — the trigger handler receives no payload (`ITriggerBus.registerTrigger` handlers take none and the scheduler job body is a fixed `{}`), so the two scopes are chosen by the handler from `IClock.now()`: every run reconciles the **at-risk set**, and the run in the configured daily hour (constant `FULL_RECONCILE_HOUR_UTC`) also runs the **full set**. Because a Pub/Sub redelivery after that hour would silently skip the day's full pass, the job records `last_full_reconcile_at` (one nullable column on `billing.provider_health`, per merchant key — a deviation from M25-S03's DDL, listed under Files and in the M25 change list) and runs the full pass whenever it is older than 24 h and the clock is at or past `FULL_RECONCILE_HOUR_UTC`. Stateless apart from that, testable with the fake clock, and it keeps the family at six scheduler jobs (seven with M27's purge, as `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` counts); this story corrects the cron table in `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing cron triggers (UC-113, UC-115, UC-116, UC-132, UC-139, UC-140) to "hourly, full pass once a day".
**Scopes (R10).** *At-risk (every hour):* open charges (`PENDING` / `OVERDUE`) of `TRIALING` subscriptions whose `trial_ends_at` is within 14 days, of `PAST_DUE` subscriptions, of `SUSPENDED` subscriptions (the open `RECOVERY` charge), **and any open charge with `due_date <= today`** (so a missed webhook is corrected before the sweep turns a paying `ACTIVE` tenant `PAST_DUE` and emails it). *Full (daily):* every open charge, plus every charge `PAID` within the last **35 days** (a card `RECEIVED` arrives about 32 days after `CONFIRMED`, and a refund or chargeback can follow a payment) through the standalone partial index `IDX_billing_charges_paid_recent` `(paid_at) WHERE status = 'PAID'` (M25-S03). Selection is keyset-paged by `(due_date | paid_at, id)` so a per-run limit never re-reads the same first rows (no starvation); a run that hits the limit is **truncated**, continues from its cursor on the next run and is not a complete pass.
For each charge: `fetchChargeStatus` (provider call, outside any transaction, through `BillingProviderRegistry` by the charge's `merchant_profile_key`) and apply the difference through `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` — the exact same paid / overdue / refunded / **chargeback** logic as the webhook. **Drift** is fixed and counted: the daily pass emits a structured log line per differing charge and the `billing_kpi_daily` counts line (S36); any difference is a warning and more than 5 in one pass is critical (S36). **Third step — provider cancels:** charges with `provider_cancel_pending = true` (set by S06 / S19 / S20 before the post-commit provider cancel, M26-D3) are retried: `cancelCharge` through the registry, flag cleared on success; if the provider reports the charge already paid (typed `ChargeAlreadyPaidError`) that charge is the payment and goes through `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`. A run **advances `provider_health.last_reconcile_ok_at` only when it is a complete (non-truncated) pass and at least one provider call succeeded** — a run with nothing to check calls `readAccountHealth` per merchant key so an empty at-risk set cannot report success while the provider is down; the freeze (S08) reads that column. Provider unreachable → the run fails and a log-based alert signal is emitted; it retries on the next run (A1). A local charge with no provider id older than 24 h → re-attempt creation through S05's retry use case, or alert (A2). **`RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase`** (single charge, tenant-scoped) is the on-demand path S15 calls from `payment-return`: one provider read + one apply, returning the updated charge; it never throws on a provider error (the page keeps polling).

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated, and the one hourly scheduler job `ikaro-cron-billing-reconcile` in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` in the same PR; `modules/pubsub` reads the catalog, so there is no per-topic `envs/*` edit — only `envs/prod` sets the `paused` flag S05 added; then the manual Foundation apply); the job is created paused in production (S39 enables it). Like S05, update the scheduler module's test fixture (`modules/scheduler/tests/resources_unit_test.tftest.hcl`, which hard-codes the cron topics) and the "all 8" text in `variables.tf`. **Live-verification check:** `get-iam-policy` populated; `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-reconcile` and observe the handler log with counts (charges checked, drift fixed, scope AT_RISK or FULL, truncated or complete). Pass = both observed.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`ReconcileChargesUseCase`** (at-risk and full scopes, plus the provider-cancel retry step), 2. **`RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase`** (on demand).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-reconcile` (publisher only; `@NoFeatureGate()` plus an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)` as the M25-S16/S20 route-completeness detector requires); the on-demand refresh is called in-process by S15, not over HTTP by itself.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none new in M26 beyond the one nullable `last_full_reconcile_at` column on `billing.provider_health` (added to M25-S03's DDL; `provider_cancel_pending` and `IDX_billing_charges_paid_recent` are also M25-S03).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/reconcile-charges.use-case.ts`, `refresh-charge-from-provider.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts` each) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/provider-health.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/repositories/typeorm-provider-health.repository.ts` (+ specs) (modify — S06 created the `ProviderHealth` aggregate, port, entity and repository for the freeze read; this story adds the `last_reconcile_ok_at` write per `merchant_profile_key`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/ports/charge-repository.port.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/repositories/typeorm-charge.repository.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/src/test/repositories/billing/in-memory-charge.repository.ts` (modify — the `…ForJob` keyset selectors: at-risk open charges, full-pass open + recently-paid charges, `provider_cancel_pending` charges; each with an `EXPLAIN` integration test)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/ports/provider-health-repository.port.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/entities/provider-health.entity.ts`, `apps/backend/src/test/builders/billing/provider-health.builder.ts`, `provider-health-entity.builder.ts` (modify — `last_full_reconcile_at`)
- `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (modify — the `billing-reconcile` cron-table row: trigger name `cron-billing-reconcile`, hourly, full pass once a day)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/http/billing-error.mapper.ts` (modify, only if a new job-path error appears)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/reconcile-trigger.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new — class `ReconcileTriggerHandler`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/cron.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify)
- `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/main.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/variables.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/tests/resources_unit_test.tftest.hcl`, `infra/terraform/pubsub-catalog.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A payment the provider confirmed but Ikaro never heard about is picked up within the hour for an at-risk tenant (daily for everyone else) and reactivates the tenant.
- [ ] A manager who returns from the payment page sees the updated state on the next poll without waiting for the webhook.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Drift matrix: provider says paid / overdue / refunded / chargeback / unchanged × local status (table-driven; a provider `overdue` is confirm-only — no local change, the S06 sweep owns it); the apply path is `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`, asserted by a spy on the shared service
  - [ ] Provider unreachable → run fails, `last_reconcile_ok_at` not advanced; a complete pass with at least one successful provider call advances it; a truncated pass does not; a pass with nothing to check calls `readAccountHealth` and advances only if it succeeds (an empty at-risk set cannot report success during an outage)
  - [ ] Keyset paging: with a per-run limit smaller than the open-charge count, consecutive runs cover every charge (no starvation)
  - [ ] Provider-cancel retry: a `provider_cancel_pending` charge is cancelled at the provider and the flag cleared; a provider that reports it already paid goes through `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`; a failing cancel stays pending for the next run
  - [ ] The full pass runs when `last_full_reconcile_at` is older than 24 h and the clock is at or past `FULL_RECONCILE_HOUR_UTC`, including on a redelivery after that hour (fake clock)
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end on the fake: drop a webhook (fake option), run reconcile, assert the charge is paid and the subscription reactivated, with `ChargePaid` published once
  - [ ] A local `PENDING` charge without a provider id older than 24 h is re-attempted
  - [ ] The at-risk scope runs every hour; the full scope runs once a day (fake clock); at-risk selects only at-risk charges (trial ending within 14 days, `PAST_DUE`, `SUSPENDED` recovery, any open charge due today or earlier), full selects open charges and charges `PAID` within 35 days (`EXPLAIN` asserted for both, standalone indexes incl. `IDX_billing_charges_paid_recent`); the job reaches `SUSPENDED` tenants with an open recovery charge (no operational-status filter)
  - [ ] An `ACTIVE` tenant whose payment webhook was missed and whose charge is due today is corrected by the at-risk pass before the sweep marks it `PAST_DUE`
  - [ ] No provider call inside a transaction (`transactional-io` passes)
  - [ ] `RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase` on an unreachable provider returns the unchanged charge, never throws
- Tenant isolation: [ ] the refresh use case is tenant-scoped (another tenant's charge id → not found); the job passes explicit `tenantId`s
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] Checkov / Tfsec clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S08 — Self-heal, provider health, suspension freeze and the `cron-billing-self-heal` trigger (UC-140)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-140 (all of it), UC-110 (the shared create-trial use case), `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § 7. Billing Context (the narrow tenant-info port and its exact-path `permittedEdges`), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.provider_health`, `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Entry point: `TenantAccessPort` (provisioning window), `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` § Billing (planned) (Provisioning, Provider incident), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned), `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § Rule 2 — Communication via Events or BFF Only, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S06 (the `ProviderHealth` repository and entity), M26-S07 (reconcile writes `provider_health`), M25-S09 (the registration repository), M25-S11 (`CreateTrialSubscriptionUseCase`), M25-S13 (the access port **and the read-only tenant-info port with its exact-path `permittedEdges` entry**, which M25 creates for the provisioning window), M26-S05 (trigger constants and `CronBillingController`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the cron route)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → four small independent steps (its own selection, no operational-status filter); **reuses** the read-only Billing → Platform tenant-info port that M25 created (exact-path `permittedEdges`, the Notification precedent `INotificationPlatformPort.getTenantInfo`) — M26 adds no new cross-context edge here.

**Description:**
Resolve the common failure modes without an operator. Hourly trigger `cron-billing-self-heal` (the doc name is `billing-self-heal`; manual publisher `POST /cron/billing-self-heal`), four steps that fail independently: **(1) Provisioning repair** — tenants older than `PROVISIONING_GRACE_MINUTES` (15) with no subscription get their trial created through `CreateTrialSubscriptionUseCase` (idempotent, `UNIQUE(tenant_id)`). **How the tenants are found without a cross-context SQL join** (`docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` describes this as an anti-join, but CLAUDE.md §8 forbids a join across contexts): this story **extends the M25-S13 tenant-info port** (`BillingPlatformPort`, existing exact-path `permittedEdges` entry) with `listTenantsCreatedBefore(cutoff, limit, after?)` — a **keyset page** ordered `created_at ASC, id ASC`, where `after` is the `(createdAt, id)` of the last row of the previous page, so a per-run limit never re-reads the same first tenants and a tenant beyond the first page is still reached (the job pages until exhausted or until its per-run cap, and resumes from the oldest unrepaired tenant on the next run; bounded work per run, not a full re-listing that starves the tail) — returning `{tenantId, countryCode, timezone, slug, createdAt, isActive}` (the key, the port's four documented tenant fields and the `isActive` field S05 added) — backed by a new read-only Platform use case `ListTenantsCreatedBeforeUseCase` (added to the same edge's `targetPaths`) that selects through a standalone index on `tenants.created_at` — and Billing then filters out the tenants that already have a subscription through its own `subscriptions` repository (`findExistingTenantIds(pageOfTenantIds)`, called per page). **Inactive tenants (`isActive = false`) are skipped, never repaired.** No second port, no new edge direction. The `isActive` field was added to the port by S05 (its A11 guard) and is simply returned here. **Known limitation, stated:** the repair runs `CreateTrialSubscriptionUseCase` without the original event's `registrationId` (the `exempt` flag now lives on the registration, which the repair cannot locate from the tenant alone, because it is linked to the tenant only when the handler completes it), so an operator-provisioned `exempt` tenant whose event was lost would get a paying trial (the owner tenant is still exempt by slug, and the operator can use UC-137 afterwards); if the tenant-info port can expose the registration reference cheaply S08 passes it, otherwise this is documented in `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` § Billing ("Provisioning"). **(1b) Release stale registrations** — a `RESERVED` `signup_registrations` row older than 1 hour (an orphan left by a failed signup step 3, S24) is released by `SignupRegistrationRepository.releaseExpiredReservations(cutoff)` (it releases operator registrations too: a tenant created from one that expired before the Billing handler completed it is repaired by (1) as a default trial, and the operator uses UC-137 if it was meant to be exempt) (M25-S09 deliberately left this sweep to M26 — `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.signup_registrations`), so the Google account's trial is not blocked by an orphan; the count is logged. **"Released" means the `RESERVED` row is deleted** (the status column has only `RESERVED | COMPLETED`). This sweep is only the **backstop**: S24 releases its own registration immediately on any failure after the reserve step through the idempotent `POST /internal/billing/registrations/{id}/release`. The release job selects through a standalone index on `(status, created_at)`-equivalent access (the table is tiny and the filter is `status = 'RESERVED' AND created_at < cutoff`; its `EXPLAIN` is asserted like the other cross-tenant jobs, adding a partial index in M25-S03 if the plan is a scan of a non-trivial table). Self-heal repair failures are alerted **log-based**: the job is stateless, so "N failures" is a log-based metric over repeated structured error logs for the same tenant id within a window (S36), not a persisted counter (A1). **(2) Provider health** — ask each provider adapter for its webhook state through the port's account-health read and webhook-queue re-enable (both are in the M25-S14 port and already listed in `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Provider port, M26-D10 — nothing to add to the port or the doc); a paused or disabled webhook raises the alert and is re-enabled once through the API (`PUT /v3/webhooks/{id} {interrupted: false}`, `docs/ASAAS_PROVIDER.md` § 4); the alert is closed only after a delivery succeeds after the call, and if the call fails or no delivery succeeds it stays raised with the runbook link (A2); the result is recorded in `billing.provider_health`. **(3) Suspension freeze** — if reconcile (S07) has not succeeded for 24 h, or the provider is unreachable, `freeze_active` is set and S06's sweep suspends nobody for non-payment; it **clears itself** after the next successful reconcile. **Flap-proof rule:** the freeze is *set* when `last_reconcile_ok_at` is older than 24 h or the health read fails on two consecutive runs; it is *cleared* only when `last_reconcile_ok_at` is newer than the freeze start **and** the current health read succeeds (S07 advances `last_reconcile_ok_at` only after a real provider call, so an empty reconcile cannot clear it). **No `provider_health` row means "not frozen"** (the row is created lazily by the first write; a fresh environment is never frozen by an absent row, and `last_reconcile_ok_at = NULL` on an existing row counts from `updated_at`); a test pins this. Tenants are never locked out because Ikaro or the provider was down, so no operator grace is needed. Nothing here raises a domain event (a repaired trial raises nothing new).

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated, the `ikaro-cron-billing-self-heal` entry in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` (plus the scheduler test fixture and `variables.tf` text, as S05) and the index migration; no per-topic `envs/*` edit — `modules/pubsub` reads the catalog; then the manual Foundation apply); schedule hourly, created paused in production (S39 enables it). **Live-verification check:** `get-iam-policy` populated; `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-self-heal` and observe the handler log with the three step results. Pass = both observed.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`RepairMissingSubscriptionsUseCase`**, 2. **`CheckProviderHealthUseCase`**, 3. **`EvaluateSuspensionFreezeUseCase`** — orchestrated by `SelfHealTriggerHandler` → `SelfHealUseCase`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-self-heal` (publisher only). The Platform tenant-info reads are in-process ports, not new public endpoints.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** one index-only migration — the standalone `created_at` index on `platform.tenants` (`AddTenantsCreatedAtIndex`, see Files); no column change, no other migration. It is a plain `CREATE INDEX`, **not** `CONCURRENTLY`: `CONCURRENTLY` cannot run inside a transaction and this codebase runs every migration under `migrationsTransactionMode "all"` (precedent and reasoning: `1748400000013-AddStartedAtIndexToChatbotSessions.ts`, `1748500000009-AddResourceIdToScheduleClosuresAndOpenings.ts`); `platform.tenants` is small and nothing is in production yet.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/self-heal.use-case.ts`, `repair-missing-subscriptions.use-case.ts`, `check-provider-health.use-case.ts`, `evaluate-suspension-freeze.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts` each) (new; handler class `SelfHealTriggerHandler`, consumer name constant in `cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`)

Billing Context, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (wherever it lists the tenant-info port; the provider-port health read / re-enable are already documented, M26-D10), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` (modify — the tenant-info port fields now include the list method; the `platform.tenants` index entry, replacing any "no change" wording for that table)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A tenant that ended up with no subscription (a lost or dead-lettered event) gets its trial within the hour, automatically.
- [ ] If the provider or Ikaro's own reconcile has been down for a day, no paying tenant is suspended; the freeze lifts by itself.
- [ ] A paused provider webhook is re-enabled by the system where the provider allows it, and the alert stays raised until a webhook delivery succeeds after the re-enable (a cleared flag is not recovery, and a wrong token keeps failing with `401`); otherwise an alert names the runbook.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each step in isolation, including: tenant younger than the window skipped; repair idempotent; freeze set at 24 h without a reconcile success and cleared after one; step failure isolation (one step failing does not stop the others)
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end on the fake: delete a subscription row, run the job, assert the trial is recreated with the tenant's country / timezone
  - [ ] With the freeze active, S06's sweep suspends nobody; after a reconcile success the freeze clears
  - [ ] `listTenantsCreatedBefore` returns exactly the documented fields (`tenantId`, `countryCode`, `timezone`, `slug`, `createdAt`, `isActive`) and nothing else, never joins `billing` tables, and its query uses the `created_at` standalone index (`EXPLAIN` asserted); a registration `RESERVED` for more than 1 hour is released, a younger one and a `COMPLETED` one are not (boundary at exactly 1 hour)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] the repair acts per tenant id; the port returns one tenant's fields only
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above

- [ ] Keyset paging: with a page size smaller than the tenant count, one run reaches a subscription-less tenant that sits beyond the first page; inactive tenants are skipped
- [ ] No `provider_health` row → not frozen; freeze flap-proof rule (set at 24 h or two failed health reads, cleared only after a real reconcile success and a successful health read)
- [ ] The release sweep deletes `RESERVED` rows older than 1 h and nothing else; the route is annotated (`@NoFeatureGate()` + explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)`)

### M26-S09 — Dunning reminders and the `cron-billing-reminders` trigger (UC-139)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-139 (all of it), `docs/DOMAIN_EVENTS.md` § BillingReminderDue, `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: Subscription (`plan_confirmed_at`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned), `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge selectors), M26-S03 (`BillingReminderDue` class), M26-S05 (trigger constants and `CronBillingController`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the cron route), M26-S06 (the `ProviderHealth` repository, read for the day-12 reminder; mermaid edge S06 → S09 to add)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → job use case emitting a `BillingReminderDue` **Command per milestone** through the outbox, deduplicated by its business `dedup_key`; consumed by S12 (M26-D6; the command's topic is created by S12's `subscribe()`). Its own standalone-index selection with no operational-status filter (overdue reminders serve `PAST_DUE` and recovery-charge tenants).

**Description:**
Warn the manager repeatedly, with dates, before anything is suspended, with no human sending reminders. Daily trigger `cron-billing-reminders` (the doc name is `billing-reminders`; manual publisher `POST /cron/billing-reminders`). For each non-exempt subscription the job publishes `BillingReminderDue` **once per milestone**, deduplicated by `(subscription, kind, daysOffset, chargeId)`: `PLAN_CONFIRMATION` at 10, 7 and 4 days before trial end while `plan_confirmed_at` is NULL (the last says the default plan will be invoiced in one day); `TRIAL_ENDING` at 7 and 3 days before trial end and on the last day (with the payment link when the first invoice exists); `CHARGE_DUE_SOON` 3 days before a charge's due date (**not** for the first trial charge — `TRIAL_ENDING` covers it the same day — and **not** for a `RECOVERY` charge — `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` covers it); `CHARGE_OVERDUE` at 1, 3, 7 and 12 days after the due date of a `PAST_DUE` subscription (the last states the suspension date); `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` for a `SUSPENDED` subscription (reasons `NON_PAYMENT` and `PAYMENT_REVERSED` only — not `CANCELED` or `OPERATOR`) whose `RECOVERY` charge is still open: 3 days after the charge is issued, and at 1 and 7 days after its due date once it is `OVERDUE` (the email says the account stays suspended until the one-month charge is paid and links the payment page; it does not quote the clean-up date, because no job acts on it before M27); it stops when the charge is paid or cancelled. Without it a suspended tenant that ignored the first recovery email would never hear from Ikaro again. Ikaro sends every reminder itself because the provider's customer notifications are off. **Deduplication needs no new column or table:** the milestone key `(subscription, kind, daysOffset, chargeId)` is the `dedup_key` of the `BillingReminderDue` **Command** in `shared.outbox` (S03; the same mechanism as `BookingReminderDue`), whose `UNIQUE` makes a second publication of the same milestone a no-op. **Freshness is Billing's job, not Notification's (R3):** Pub/Sub is at-least-once with no delivery-time guarantee and Notification has no Billing read port, so the job re-checks **immediately before writing the outbox row** that the charge is still open (`PENDING` / `OVERDUE`) and that the subscription is not `exempt`; it never publishes a command for an exempt subscription or for a paid or cancelled charge (A1). A small stale window between publish and send is accepted, and the reminder copy is tolerant ("Se você já pagou, desconsidere este aviso"). No recipient → logged, the dashboard banner remains (A2). The command carries the nullable `recipientEmail` that Billing resolved (S03); S12 falls back to the managers when it is null. **Dates:** the `CHARGE_OVERDUE` day-12 reminder states the suspension date computed from the sweep's own rule (`grace_until` when set, else `past_due_since` + 14 days) in the tenant timezone, and is not sent while the suspension freeze is active for the subscription's merchant key. Milestones match the **exact** `daysOffset`; a missed cron day loses that milestone permanently (accepted and stated). "Business days" anywhere in the reminder rules means Mon-Fri with no holiday calendar in M26 (known limitation).

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated, the `ikaro-cron-billing-reminders` entry in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` plus the scheduler test fixture and `variables.tf` text as S05 (no per-topic `envs/*` edit); then the manual Foundation apply); schedule daily, created paused in production (S39 enables it). **Live-verification check:** `get-iam-policy` populated; `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-reminders`, then observe the handler log and, with a staging subscription 7 days from trial end, one `BillingReminderDue` audit-log line. Pass = IAM + both logs.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`PublishBillingRemindersUseCase`** (job), per-subscription milestone evaluation as a pure domain service `ReminderSchedule` (testable without a database).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-reminders` (publisher only).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none (deduplication is the outbox `dedup_key`).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/reminder-schedule.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/publish-billing-reminders.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` (modify only if it still lists fewer than five reminder kinds after the audit fix; UC-139 and the `BillingReminderDue` entry in DOMAIN_EVENTS already have all five, including `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN`, so nothing to add there)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/reminders-trigger.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new — class `RemindersTriggerHandler`, consumer name in `cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/cron.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/ports/subscription-repository.port.ts`, `charge-repository.port.ts`, their TypeORM adapters and in-memory doubles (modify — the `…ForJob` reminder selectors: trialing by `trial_ends_at`, open charges by due date, `PAST_DUE`, `SUSPENDED` with open recovery; each with an `EXPLAIN` integration test)
- `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/main.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/variables.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/tests/resources_unit_test.tftest.hcl`, `infra/terraform/pubsub-catalog.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A trial tenant gets plan-confirmation reminders at 10, 7 and 4 days, trial-ending warnings at 7 and 3 days and on the last day; a due charge gets a reminder 3 days before; an overdue one at 1, 3, 7 and 12 days (the last names the suspension date) — each exactly once.
- [ ] Nobody is reminded about a charge they already paid (Billing re-checks at publish time; a payment landing in the short window before the email is read is covered by the tolerant copy).
- [ ] The first trial charge gets `TRIAL_ENDING` only (no same-day `CHARGE_DUE_SOON`), and a recovery charge gets `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` only.
- [ ] A suspended tenant (non-payment or reversed payment) with an open recovery charge is reminded 3 days after issue and at 1 and 7 days after its due date, each once; nothing is sent for a `CANCELED` or `OPERATOR` suspension.
- [ ] Exempt tenants receive nothing.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] `ReminderSchedule` table-driven over every milestone and boundary (UTC-3 and DST tenants), including "plan confirmed → no `PLAN_CONFIRMATION`"
  - [ ] Exempt skipped; handler calls exactly one use case and passes `correlationId`
  - [ ] Publish-time re-check: a charge paid or cancelled, or a subscription turned exempt, between selection and the outbox write publishes nothing; the day-12 reminder's suspension date honours `grace_until`
  - [ ] `CHARGE_DUE_SOON` is not produced for the first trial charge nor for a `RECOVERY` charge; a skipped cron day does not back-fill the missed milestone
- Integration:
  - [ ] Running the job twice on one day publishes each milestone once (the outbox `dedup_key` holds under concurrency)
  - [ ] End to end through the in-memory bus on the fake clock: advance time day by day and assert the exact sequence of commands for one trial and one overdue subscription
  - [ ] Selection uses standalone indexes (`EXPLAIN`)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] reminders carry their own `tenantId`; one run covers several tenants without crossing data
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] Checkov / Tfsec clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean
