### M26-S10 — `provider_events` retention purge and the `cron-billing-provider-events-purge` trigger (UC-114)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** S
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-114 (the retention job row), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.provider_events`, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing cron triggers, `docs/DATA_RETENTION_AND_PRIVACY.md` (retention conventions), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_TESTING.md` § Standalone index for a cross-tenant system job, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (the `ProviderEvent` model, port and repository this story extends), M26-S04 (the table is populated), M26-S05 (trigger constants and `CronBillingController`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the cron route)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → retention use case with a bounded batch delete (precedent: `LeadFormRetentionPurgeTriggerHandler`).

**Description:**
Keep the webhook inbox small. Daily trigger `cron-billing-provider-events-purge` (the doc name is `billing-provider-events-purge`; manual publisher `POST /cron/billing-provider-events-purge`) deletes `provider_events` older than 30 days in bounded batches through the standalone index on `received_at` (created in M25-S03). `PurgeProviderEventsUseCase` takes no tenant (the table is processed globally; each row carries its resolved `tenant_id` and event type but no payload or personal data), deletes in batches of a fixed size and stops at a per-run limit. Idempotent by nature. Postgres `DELETE` has no `LIMIT`, so each batch is `DELETE FROM billing.provider_events WHERE (provider, event_id) IN (SELECT provider, event_id FROM billing.provider_events WHERE received_at < $1 ORDER BY received_at LIMIT $2)` (the inner select uses the `received_at` index). (The 30-day figure is the canonical retention; the provider's own 14-day expiry is why reconcile exists.)

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated, the `ikaro-cron-billing-provider-events-purge` entry in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` plus the scheduler test fixture and `variables.tf` text as S05 (no per-topic `envs/*` edit); then the manual Foundation apply); schedule daily, created paused in production (S39 enables it). **Live-verification check:** `get-iam-policy` populated and one manual `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-provider-events-purge` observed in the handler log. Pass = both observed.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`PurgeProviderEventsUseCase`**.
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-provider-events-purge` (publisher only; `@NoFeatureGate()` plus an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)`).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/purge-provider-events.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `docs/DATA_RETENTION_AND_PRIVACY.md` (modify — the "Planned: Billing data retention" section already exists with the `provider_events` 30-day row; flip that row from planned to implemented. The fiscal-retention years stay open until the accountant answers, S39 records them; S24 adds the signup and billing-account personal data)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/provider-events-purge-trigger.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new — class `ProviderEventsPurgeTriggerHandler`, consumer name constant in `cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/cron.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/ports/provider-event-repository.port.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/repositories/typeorm-provider-event.repository.ts` (+ specs) and the in-memory provider-event repository double (modify — `deleteOlderThanBatch`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify)
- `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/main.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/variables.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/tests/resources_unit_test.tftest.hcl`, `infra/terraform/pubsub-catalog.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] Webhook records older than 30 days disappear; newer ones stay.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit: [ ] boundary exactly 30 days (kept) vs 30 days + 1 second (deleted); batch loop stops at the per-run limit
- Integration: [ ] deletes only old rows; `EXPLAIN` uses the `received_at` index; running twice is safe
- Tenant isolation: none — the purge is a documented cross-tenant system job; rows carry a `tenant_id` but nothing tenant-visible is returned or modified beyond deletion by age
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above

- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code

### M26-S11 — Notification: platform-scoped template lookup and the Billing templates (UC-124 precondition)

**Agent:** `backend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-124 (preconditions: no per-tenant copy, no backfill), `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § 3. Notification Context and § Communication Patterns (the event-to-email map), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § Schema: `notification` (`notification_templates`, `tenant_id` nullability), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Adding a new notification type (steps 1–7), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Authoring new i18n UI copy keys, `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md` (plain `ALTER TABLE`)
**Dependencies:** M25-S23 (the notification gate, its `bypassSuspensionGate` mapping flag and the status CHECK; Billing templates must be exempt from it)
**Pattern:** The existing five-file notification-type recipe (enum → mapping → both `notifications.json` → migration → send use case) plus **one explicit key family**: `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS`, a domain constant listing the Billing keys. The family is the least machinery that keeps platform rows out of the per-tenant default copy and is **flagged as a judgment call for story discovery**.

**Description:**
Billing emails are platform-level — UC-124: "no per-tenant copy and no backfill". **A trap the earlier draft missed:** in Notification, rows with `tenant_id IS NULL` are the *global defaults*, and `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` (called when a tenant is provisioned, and by `SeedDefaultTemplatesUseCase` / `seed.ts`) copies **every** `tenant_id IS NULL` row for the locale into the new tenant. Seeding the Billing templates as plain NULL rows would copy them into every tenant. The mechanism, with no schema change: (1) add the Billing keys to `NotificationTemplateKey` (`notification-template-key.enum.ts`) and their entries to `notification-template-key.mapping.ts` — **each entry is `{eventName, recipientType, variables, bypassSuspensionGate: true, platformScoped: true}`**: `variables` is the placeholder list the mapping spec compares with `notifications.json` in both languages (docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md "every placeholder in a template must be supplied"), `bypassSuspensionGate` is the flag M25-S23 defines on the mapping so the notification gate lets the template through while the tenant is suspended, and `platformScoped` marks the family (the two flags are added to the mapping's entry type in this story if M25-S23 has not already added `bypassSuspensionGate`). **One template key per wording** (docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md "wording that varies by actor or reason is a separate template key, not a sentence variable"; the localization catalog holds whole `{subject, body}` templates addressed by `{eventName}.{recipientType}`, so a `kind`-style variable would hard-code copy in code — M23-S28 reversed exactly that). The keys (the enum value is the kebab-case of the name; `eventName.recipientType` in `notifications.json`): `CHARGE_ISSUED_PERIOD`, `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY`, `CHARGE_PAID`, `SUBSCRIPTION_PAST_DUE`, `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_NON_PAYMENT` (reasons `NON_PAYMENT` and `PAYMENT_REVERSED`), `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_OPERATOR` (no payment instructions, "contact Ikaro"), `SUBSCRIPTION_REACTIVATED`, `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_IMMEDIATE`, `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_SCHEDULED`, `PLAN_CHANGED`, `BILLING_REMINDER_PLAN_CONFIRMATION`, `BILLING_REMINDER_TRIAL_ENDING`, `BILLING_REMINDER_CHARGE_DUE_SOON`, `BILLING_REMINDER_CHARGE_OVERDUE`, `BILLING_REMINDER_RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN`, `SUBSCRIPTION_PRICE_CHANGE_ANNOUNCED`, and (reserved, shipped now so M27 needs no template migration) `TENANT_PURGE_WARNING` — **17 keys**. There is no key for `SubscriptionSuspended` with reason `CANCELED`: the manager gets the `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_*` email for a cancel and S12 sends nothing for that suspension reason. S12 selects the key from event data (`kind`, `reason`, `immediate`, `ChargeIssued.kind`). The `ChargeIssued` trial-fallback email states only the total and due date (the event carries no line items); (2) the domain constant `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS` is **derived from the `platformScoped` flag of the mapping** (`Object.entries(NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_KEY_MAPPING).filter(...)`), never a second hand-kept list; (3) `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` (the repository method, called only by `SeedDefaultTemplatesUseCase`) excludes the platform-scoped trigger events (`AND trigger_event <> ALL($3)`), so no tenant ever receives a copy — a unit/integration test proves it; **`apps/backend/src/shared/database/seed.ts` does not call that method**: it has two raw `INSERT … SELECT` copies of every `tenant_id IS NULL` row (`seedNotificationTemplates` and `seedTeamTenant`) which get the same exclusion, so the E2E seed tenants never receive Billing rows (the keys come from the notification domain constant, or the seed lists the excluded `trigger_event` values — `shared/database` may not import a context domain file without an architecture-policy exception, so use the explicit list there with a spec that compares it to the constant); (4) a new, explicit repository lookup `findPlatformTemplate(key, locale)` reads only `tenant_id IS NULL AND trigger_event = ANY(platform-scoped)`; the existing tenant-strict lookup is unchanged, so no existing send path can fall back to a global template by accident; (5) copy in **both** `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/notifications.json` and `…/en/notifications.json` (the migration\'s `buildSeedRows()` throws if either locale lacks a key) — a complete, shippable sentence set in both languages (never `TODO`); (6) one data migration inserts the platform rows (idempotent via `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on `UQ_notification_templates_global`; on a brand-new database `1748100000010-CreateNotificationTemplates` already seeds every enum value, so this migration is then a no-op), registered in `integration-global-setup.ts` and listed in `PERSISTENCE_BYPASS_IGNORES` (`apps/backend/eslint.config.js`). A missing platform template at send time fails loudly (UC-124 A1): a structured error log carrying the fixed field `event: "billing_template_missing"` (a log-based metric, which S36's alert policy reads; no app-defined OTel instrument, `docs/OBSERVABILITY_STRATEGY.md`) — decided: yes, an alert, because billing emails are money-critical and a template can be absent at runtime (`docs/OBSERVABILITY_STRATEGY.md` § 7. Dashboards, alerts and uptime checks (it already lists the policy)) — never a silent drop. Every Billing mapping entry carries `bypassSuspensionGate: true` (the flag M25-S23 owns; family membership alone is not the mechanism) so M25-S23\'s gate lets them through while suspended.

**Backend use case steps:** none (lookup + data).
**Backend HTTP surface:** none.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** one data migration `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/migrations/<timestamp>-SeedBillingTemplates.ts`; the 17 notification entries above in both `notifications.json` files (`eventName.recipientType`, e.g. `ChargeIssued.managerPeriod` / `ChargeIssued.managerRecovery`, `SubscriptionSuspended.managerNonPayment` / `.managerOperator`, `BillingReminderDue.planConfirmation` … `.recoveryChargeOpen`).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/domain/notification-template-key.enum.ts`, `notification-template-key.mapping.ts` (+ `notification-template-key.mapping.spec.ts`) (modify — the 17 new keys, each entry with `variables`, `bypassSuspensionGate: true`, `platformScoped: true` (the entry type gains `platformScoped`, and `bypassSuspensionGate` unless M25-S23 added it); the `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS` constant derived from `platformScoped` and exported from the mapping file; the mapping spec gains "every platform-scoped key bypasses the suspension gate" and the existing variable/placeholder parity covers the new copy)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/ports/notification-template-repository.port.ts` (modify — `findPlatformTemplate`; `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` excludes the family)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/repositories/typeorm-notification-template.repository.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/test/repositories/notification/in-memory-notification-template.repository.ts` (modify — same two behaviours)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/seed-default-templates/seed-default-templates.use-case.ts` (+ spec; its only call to `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant`, asserted to copy none of the family) and `apps/backend/src/shared/database/seed.ts` (modify — its two raw `INSERT … SELECT` copies, `seedNotificationTemplates` and `seedTeamTenant`, exclude the platform-scoped `trigger_event` values; the seed does not call the repository method). **The exclusion is applied in all three SQLs**: the repository's `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` (`WHERE tenant_id IS NULL AND locale = $2`, repository :62) and both raw seed copies (seed.ts :401 and :449, which select **all** `tenant_id IS NULL` rows with no locale column); the integration spec covers each of the three
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/migrations/<timestamp>-SeedBillingTemplates.ts` (new)
- `apps/backend/src/test/integration-global-setup.ts` (modify — register the migration), `apps/backend/eslint.config.js` (modify — `PERSISTENCE_BYPASS_IGNORES` entry)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/notifications.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/notifications.json` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/test/builders/notification/notification-template.builder.ts` and `notification-template-entity.builder.ts` (modify — a `withPlatformScope()` default)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] Every Billing email exists in both languages with complete, readable copy; no existing tenant email changes, and **no tenant ever receives a copy of a Billing template**.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Every enum key has a mapping entry with `variables` (the existing specs); every platform-scoped entry has `bypassSuspensionGate: true`; `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS` equals the set of `platformScoped` entries (17); `findPlatformTemplate` returns the platform row for the requested locale, missing → typed error; the tenant-strict lookup never returns a platform row
- Integration:
  - [ ] The migration seeds each template in both locales exactly once and is idempotent
  - [ ] **Provisioning a new tenant copies none of the platform-scoped templates** (`copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` via `SeedDefaultTemplatesUseCase`, and the two raw copies in `seed.ts`) while still copying every existing default
  - [ ] Existing notification integration suites pass unchanged
- Tenant isolation: [ ] a tenant template with the same key is never returned by the platform lookup, and the platform row is never returned by the tenant lookup
- E2E: none — covered by integration
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S12 — Billing email handlers (UC-124)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-124 and UC-139 (A1: re-check before sending), `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § Communication Patterns (the event-to-email map), `docs/DOMAIN_EVENTS.md` § Billing Context Events, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Event Handlers (Pub/Sub consumers) and § Adding a new notification type, `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md` § A domain event is drained, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` § Billing configuration (planned), `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (subscription on an existing topic)
**Dependencies:** M26-S03 (the events and topics), M26-S09 (`BillingReminderDue`), M26-S11 (templates and the lookup), M25-S21b (the mandatory `skipWhenSuspended` declaration on every `subscribe()`), M25-S23 (the gate; Billing templates exempt), M25-S28 (the Notification → Billing event edges, same pattern)
**Pattern:** One event handler per event, each calling exactly one send use case (`BaseNotificationUseCase` Template Method); idempotent on `eventId`; global-template lookup (S11).

**Description:**
Ikaro sends every Billing email itself (Asaas customers are created with `notificationDisabled = true`). For each of `ChargeIssued` (payment link), `ChargePaid` (receipt link), `SubscriptionPastDue`, `SubscriptionSuspended`, `SubscriptionReactivated`, `SubscriptionCancelled`, `PlanChanged`, `BillingReminderDue` (all five kinds) and `SubscriptionPriceChangeAnnounced` (one consolidated email per tenant: "o valor de X passa de R$ 39 para R$ 49 a partir de 10/12; seu novo total mensal será R$ …"), a handler class (names unique across the codebase) subscribes with its own consumer name and calls one send use case, passing `event.correlationId`. **Every one of the nine `subscribe()` calls declares `{ skipWhenSuspended: false }`** (M25-S21b\'s explicit-declaration detector fails CI without a declaration; `true` would make the dispatcher skip `SubscriptionSuspended` and the recovery `ChargeIssued` for exactly the suspended tenant and defeat the acceptance criterion below). **Data source — events only:** the recipients, the payment link and the receipt link are in the event data (`ChargeIssued.paymentUrl`, `ChargePaid` receipt URL — added in S03); the recipient is the event's nullable `recipientEmail` (resolved by Billing, S03 — the single documented exception to "no email in payload"); when it is null Notification falls back to the tenant's MANAGER emails through the existing `INotificationStaffPort.getManagerEmails`, and reads locale and tenant details through the existing `INotificationPlatformPort` — **no Billing read port and no new cross-context edge in the Notification → Billing direction beyond the event classes**, each of which needs an exact-path `permittedEdges` entry. **Recipients:** the event's `recipientEmail` if non-null, else the tenant's managers (`INotificationStaffPort.getManagerEmails`). **Freshness and exempt tenants are Billing's responsibility, not Notification's (R3):** Pub/Sub is at-least-once with no delivery-time guarantee and Notification has no Billing read port, so **Notification does no re-check and no exempt check**. Billing re-checks "charge still open" and "subscription not exempt" immediately before writing the outbox row of any email-triggering event or command, never publishes an email-triggering event for an `exempt` subscription, and events raised from exempt paths (for example the set-exempt hook's `SubscriptionReactivated`) are not emailed: Billing does not publish the email-triggering event at all for those paths (the use case that sets the exempt flag decides this before the outbox write; S20 fills that hook). A small stale window is accepted; reminder and `ChargeIssued` copy is tolerant ("Se você já pagou, desconsidere este aviso"). Redelivery is deduplicated on `eventId` via the notification log (A3); a delivery failure uses the existing failed / retry handling (A2); a missing platform template alerts (A1). Billing emails use the platform lookup (S11) and are exempt from the suspension gate (M25-S23). **Consumer names and the suspended pair.** `SubscriptionSuspended` and `SubscriptionReactivated` already have M25-S28's customer-notice consumer (`notify-customers-tenant-unavailable`, the reason-neutral email to customers); this story's manager handlers use their own distinct consumer names (`send-subscription-suspended-notification`, `send-subscription-reactivated-notification`) and their own push subscriptions — two independent consumers of one topic, never merged. **One template key per wording (S11).** The handler/use case picks the key from event data: `ChargeIssued.kind` → `CHARGE_ISSUED_PERIOD` or `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY`; `SubscriptionCancelled.immediate` → `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_IMMEDIATE` or `_SCHEDULED`; `BillingReminderDue.kind` → the matching `BILLING_REMINDER_*` key; `SubscriptionSuspended.reason` as below. **What the manager email does by suspension reason** (read from the event's reason; product default, DEFERRABLE): `NON_PAYMENT` / `PAYMENT_REVERSED` → `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_NON_PAYMENT`: "your account is suspended, pay one month to reactivate", linking the **billing page** (the dashboard path `/dashboard/billing`, reachable while suspended) — **not** a payment link, because the recovery charge is created at the provider after commit and the suspension event cannot carry its URL; the email says the payment link follows in the next email (the recovery `ChargeIssued`, `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY`, from S06), and points to "Dados de cobrança" when the billing address is missing (a held recovery charge has no `ChargeIssued`, R1); `CANCELED` → **no manager email from this handler** (the manager gets exactly one email per cancel: `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_*`; the same for a UC-142 deactivation); `OPERATOR` → `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_OPERATOR`: the manager is still told, with **no payment link and no reactivation instructions** (only the operator can lift it; there is no recovery charge), saying to contact Ikaro. The purge-warning email (`TenantPurgeWarning`) is **not** wired here — M27 owns its handler (its template ships in S11).

**Devops half (nine new subscriptions on the S03 / M25-S15b topics, and the `BillingReminderDue` command\'s topic, which this story\'s `subscribe()` creates).** **PR sequence:** 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply per the playbook row "A new Pub/Sub topic (… cron-published `Command` with a consumer …) + its app code" (the README has no separate row for a subscription on an existing topic: row 2 covers the `subscribe()` call site and its subscription) — PR1 (`infra-app-mix-ok`): the handlers' `subscribe()` call sites, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated (subscriptions and their DLQs are derived from the catalog by `modules/pubsub`; no per-subscription `envs/*` edit, as M25-S11 records); then the manual Foundation apply. **IAM / permissions:** push-subscription invoker per subscription, derived from the catalog; no new principal. **Live-verification check:** on staging after the apply: `gcloud pubsub subscriptions describe <each>` shows the push endpoint; then provision a throwaway staging tenant with a manager email you own, advance time and issue a charge (precondition: S13's `simulate` / `seed` endpoints are merged — S12 does not depend on S13; otherwise use M25-S12's clock endpoint plus a manual `gcloud scheduler jobs run` of the issue job, or a one-off backend REPL call publishing the event), and confirm one `ChargeIssued` email arrives (Mailhog is local only; on staging the tenant manager's own inbox) and the notification log row is `SENT`. Pass = subscriptions described + one email delivered.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. One `Send<Event>NotificationUseCase` per event (nine, thin, over `BaseNotificationUseCase`), 2. one handler class per event under `contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/` **or** `contexts/notification/infrastructure/events/` — the Notification context owns sending; the handlers live in **Notification** (consumers of Billing events, listed in `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § Communication Patterns), subscribing with consumer names `send-<event>-notification`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — event-driven.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-charge-issued-notification/send-charge-issued-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-charge-paid-notification/send-charge-paid-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-past-due-notification/send-subscription-past-due-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-suspended-notification/send-subscription-suspended-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-reactivated-notification/send-subscription-reactivated-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-cancelled-notification/send-subscription-cancelled-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-plan-changed-notification/send-plan-changed-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-billing-reminder-notification/send-billing-reminder-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-price-change-announced-notification/send-price-change-announced-notification.use-case.ts` (+ `.use-case.spec.ts` and `.use-case.integration.spec.ts` beside each) (new — nine folders following the existing `send-booking-*` sibling folders)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/events/charge-issued-notification.handler.ts`, `charge-paid-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-past-due-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-suspended-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-reactivated-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-cancelled-notification.handler.ts`, `plan-changed-notification.handler.ts`, `billing-reminder-notification.handler.ts`, `price-change-announced-notification.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts` each) (new — class names are the PascalCase of the file names, unique across the codebase)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/notification.module.ts` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/dtos/send-charge-issued-notification.dto.ts` and one sibling DTO per use case (new), with matching DTO builders in `apps/backend/src/test/builders/notification/` (new, following the `send-booking-*-dto.builder.ts` siblings; exported from its `index.ts`)
- `infra/terraform/pubsub-catalog.json` (modify — regenerated; no `envs/*` edit)
- `packages/architecture-check/architecture-policy.json` (modify — one exact-path `permittedEdges` entry per handler importing a Billing event class or the `BillingReminderDue` command, nine entries, source = the handler file, target = `billing/domain/events/<event>.event.ts` or `billing/domain/commands/billing-reminder-due.command.ts`; the precedent is the 22 existing Notification entries such as `booking-approved.handler.ts` → `booking`)
- `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` (modify only if an implemented consumer name differs)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] The manager (or the billing email) receives the right email, in their language, for each billing event, once; exempt tenants receive none (Billing never publishes for them, R3).
- [ ] A manager gets exactly one email for a cancel (the `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_*` one; none for the `CANCELED` suspension).
- [ ] A reminder or invoice email whose charge was paid in the meantime carries tolerant wording ("Se você já pagou, desconsidere este aviso"); the freshness check itself is Billing's, at publish time (S09 / S05).

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each use case: recipient resolution (event `recipientEmail` vs the managers fallback; the `OPERATOR` key without a payment link), template-key selection from event data (`ChargeIssued.kind`, `SubscriptionCancelled.immediate`, `SubscriptionSuspended.reason`, `BillingReminderDue.kind`), `reason = CANCELED` sends nothing, locale, variables per template; no Billing read and no re-check in Notification (the use case has no Billing port)
  - [ ] Each handler calls exactly one use case, rethrows, passes `correlationId`, and its `subscribe()` declares `skipWhenSuspended: false` (the M25-S21b detector passes)
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end through the in-memory bus on the fake: each event → one log row `SENT`; redelivery of the same `eventId` creates no second row
  - [ ] Missing platform template → failure plus a structured error log with the fixed field `event: "billing_template_missing"` (the log-based metric S36's alert reads), not a silent drop
  - [ ] A suspended tenant still receives its `SubscriptionSuspended` / `ChargeIssued (recovery)` email (the gate exemption); an `OPERATOR` suspension sends the manager email without a payment link, and M25-S28's customer notice is unaffected and independent
- Tenant isolation: [ ] emails go only to the event's tenant's recipients; the log row carries that `tenantId`
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] Checkov / Tfsec clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S13 — Dev `simulate` and `seed` endpoints (UC-138)

**Agent:** `backend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-138 (all of it), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Provider Webhooks and Internal API (non-production endpoints), `docs/TESTING_STRATEGY.md` § Testing the billing provider — fake, sandbox, contract suite and clock, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` § Billing configuration (planned), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_TESTING.md` § Billing provider tests (planned)
**Dependencies:** M26-S04 (the backend internal webhook route the fake delivers to), M26-S05 (charge issuing), M26-S06 (statuses), M25-S12 (clock, the dev controller pattern and the `APP_ENV` rule), M25-S14 (the fake's delivery options and `BillingProviderRegistry`), M25-S15a (`PlatformAdminGuard` in shared), M25-S09 / M25-S10 (the registrations and `POST /internal/tenants` routes the seed script calls; `exempt` is a field of the OPERATOR registration), M25-S15b (`SetTenantExemptUseCase`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the dev routes)
**Pattern:** Test-support endpoints registered only in `local` / `staging`; the normal path processes every simulated event (**no test-only path inside any use case**).

**Description:**
Let a developer or operator drive every billing state without a provider account or waiting. `POST /internal/dev/billing/provider/simulate` `{ tenantId | chargeId, event, delivery, failure? }` — `event ∈ PAID_PIX | PAID_BOLETO | PAID_CARD | OVERDUE | REFUND_FULL | REFUND_PARTIAL | CHARGEBACK_OPENED | CHARGEBACK_LOST`; `delivery ∈ NORMAL | DUPLICATE | DELAYED | OUT_OF_ORDER`; `failure ∈ PROVIDER_5XX | LINK_EXPIRED | INVALID_LOCATION` (fails the next provider call). The `FakeBillingProvider` builds the webhook payload (with the verified per-method sequences) and **delivers it in-process / by loopback to the backend internal webhook route `POST /internal/billing/webhooks/{provider}` (S04, `InternalApiGuard`)** — not to the BFF public route: the static `asaas-access-token` is verified only by the BFF (a BFF-only secret, S01/S04), so the BFF token check is covered by S04's own BFF tests and `simulate` exercises everything behind it (normalisation, inbox, `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`); the normal path processes it, no test-only path exists in any use case. **Which charge:** with `chargeId` that charge; with `tenantId` the tenant's single open charge for the `PAID_*` / `OVERDUE` events and its most recent `PAID` charge for `REFUND_*` / `CHARGEBACK_*`; exactly one of `tenantId` / `chargeId` is required (`400` otherwise). **When the event does not apply** (a paid event for a charge that is not open, a refund for a charge that is not paid, no matching charge) the endpoint returns `409` with a Problem Details body and delivers nothing. `DELAYED` and `OUT_OF_ORDER` deliveries are produced by the fake as a sequence delivered within the request (the delayed one after a short in-request wait, the out-of-order pair reversed), never by a background timer. Works **only** for a subscription whose `merchant_profile_key` is a `*_FAKE` key (A2: sandbox / live keys → refused; staging sandboxes are driven by the provider's own sandbox). **Seed is a script, not a Billing-owned provisioning path.** Creating a tenant needs Platform provisioning, the signup registration and Staff, and M26 adds no Billing → Platform provisioning edge (milestone "Cross-context edges"). So `scripts/billing-seed.sh` (the `scripts/provision-tenant.sh` precedent) **orchestrates HTTP**: `POST /internal/billing/registrations` (with `exempt: true` for the exempt state), `POST /internal/tenants` (with the returned `registrationId`), `POST /internal/dev/clock/advance`, and `POST /internal/dev/billing/provider/simulate` plus the cron publishers (`POST /cron/billing-issue-charges`, `…/billing-status-sweep` — the manual route keeps the short name, the trigger constant is `cron-billing-*`) to reach each state — one tenant per state with **fixed slugs** (`billing-seed-trialing`, `-active`, `-past-due`, `-suspended`, `-exempt`), so a re-run finds the existing tenants and only advances what is missing (re-runnable). The `exempt` state may also be reached through `SetTenantExemptUseCase` (M25-S15b). The backend endpoint `POST /internal/dev/billing/seed` only drives the **Billing-side** steps of that sequence (`SeedBillingStatesUseCase`: given an existing seeded tenant id and a target state, advance charges and statuses through the normal use cases); it creates no tenant. States: `TRIALING`, `ACTIVE`, `PAST_DUE`, `SUSPENDED` for `NON_PAYMENT`, `exempt`. **The US variants (US tenant with and without address, UC-138) are M28** and are not seeded here; record that in the milestone Coverage / Non-Goals. The two states that need a cancel — `ACTIVE` with a scheduled cancel and `SUSPENDED` for `CANCELED` — are added by **S20**, which extends `SeedBillingStatesUseCase` and the script (S13 cannot reach them without S20's cancel use case and deliberately takes no S20 dependency). Both endpoints sit behind `InternalApiGuard` + `PlatformAdminGuard`, are registered **only when `APP_ENV ∈ {local, staging}`** and do not exist (404) in production (A1); missing keys → `401` (A3). **Production safety:** `APP_ENV` defaults to `local` in `env.validation.ts`, so registration also requires an explicit positive signal and a **startup test refuses production-like configuration** (for example `NODE_ENV=production`, or a live `merchant_profile_key` configured) with `APP_ENV` unset or `local`; the production Terraform sets `APP_ENV = "production"` (`infra/terraform/envs/prod/main.tf`). On `staging`, `simulate` is effectively unavailable (staging subscriptions use `BRL_ASAAS_SANDBOX`, only `local` has `*_FAKE` keys) and `seed` runs against the Asaas sandbox with the single-instance dev clock (M25-S12 rule). Scripts `scripts/billing-simulate.sh` and `scripts/billing-seed.sh` wrap them.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`SimulateProviderEventUseCase`** (fake only), 2. **`SeedBillingStatesUseCase`**.
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends/creates `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-dev-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /internal/dev/billing/provider/simulate`, `POST /internal/dev/billing/seed` (`InternalApiGuard` + `PlatformAdminGuard`, `@NoFeatureGate()` plus an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)`; module-conditional: registered only when `APP_ENV` is `local` or `staging`).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/simulate-provider-event.use-case.ts`, `seed-billing-states.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-dev-billing.controller.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/http/billing/dev-billing.http` (new)
- `scripts/billing-simulate.sh`, `scripts/billing-seed.sh` (new — the seed script orchestrates the HTTP calls listed in the description; no Billing → Platform edge, so `architecture-policy.json` is not touched)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/providers/fake-billing.provider.ts` (modify — the delivery to the backend internal webhook route, if M25-S14 left it as a builder only)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify — conditional registration, plus the startup test's guard)
- `docs/TESTING_STRATEGY.md`, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (modify only to say `simulate` delivers to the backend internal route and `seed` is script-orchestrated)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A developer can put a local tenant into each billing state (the two cancel states once S20 lands) and fire each provider event (including duplicated and out-of-order) in one command; production cannot.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Controllers are registered only when `APP_ENV` is `local` or `staging` and not registered for any other value (production included); a production-like configuration with `APP_ENV` unset or `local` fails startup; `simulate` refused for a non-`*_FAKE` key
  - [ ] `simulate` input: exactly one of `tenantId` / `chargeId` (`400` otherwise); charge selection by event kind with `tenantId`; a non-applicable event → `409` and nothing delivered
- Integration:
  - [ ] Each `event` × `delivery` combination produces exactly the state a real event would (reusing the S04 assertions through the HTTP path)
  - [ ] The seed script, run twice, leaves one tenant per documented state under the fixed slugs (the two cancel states arrive with S20) and creates them only through normal use cases / routes; the Billing-side endpoint creates no tenant
  - [ ] Missing / wrong operator keys → `401`; production-configured app context → `404`
- Tenant isolation: [ ] `simulate` targets one tenant's subscription/charge; a charge id of another tenant is not found
- E2E: none — covered by integration (S35 uses these endpoints)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

## Wave 2 — Manager backend / BFF

> The manager endpoints share one BFF controller (`apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, created in M25-S13 for `GET /v1/billing/access`) with sibling `billing.schemas.ts` / `billing.types.ts` / `billing.mapper.ts`, and one backend controller family under `contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/`. Every BFF route is `@Roles('MANAGER')`, `@NoFeatureGate()` (no sellable feature owns the billing surface) and `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (M25-S16), so a suspended manager can reach it. **The backend `billing.controller.ts` carries the same `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` on every route** (the M25-S16 global `TenantAccessGuard` also runs in the backend and defaults an unannotated route to suspension-denied; the `tenant-access-route-metadata` detector is enforcing after M25-S24) and reads the actor role from `X-Actor-Role`; each story below therefore lists the decorator specs in its AC. Tenant scope comes from the JWT and `RequestContext`, never a request field. **Error-code ownership (M26 rule):** the story that first throws a `BILLING_*` code owns its `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` member, both `packages/i18n/locales/{pt-BR,en}/errors.json` entries, the error mapper and, for BFF-thrown codes, `BffThrowableCode` (`apps/bff/src/shared/http/problem-detail.ts`); M25-S06 translated only `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_REQUIRED`, `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_INVALID` and `BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID`. Because they edit the same BFF files, **the Wave 2 BFF stories are sequential in practice** (see `## Run-batch preview`).

### M26-S14 — Manager reads — summary and charge history (UC-117), plus the operator list's Charge-derived fields (UC-111)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-117 (all), UC-130 (banner states), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (BFF, role MANAGER) (summary response, charge history item, placement and gating), `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Context 7: Billing Context (`suspendsAt`, `purgeAt`, `paymentConfirmationPending`), `docs/BFF_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Module & Controller Naming Conventions, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Base Standards (Pattern A pagination, money)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge repository, listing), M25-S13 (BFF billing module and `@NoFeatureGate`/suspension-policy wiring), M25-S16 (`@SuspensionAccess`)
**Pattern:** Query use cases (read models) + BFF mapper shaping the response (enums, no display strings); Pattern A pagination.

**Description:**
The read side of the billing page. `GET /v1/billing/summary` returns the documented shape: `status`, `exempt`, `suspensionReason` (only while `SUSPENDED`), `currency`/`countryCode`, `timezone` (the subscription's immutable IANA zone, so the web formats instants in it, never in the browser zone), `cycle` (always `MONTHLY` in M26) with `nextCycle` / `nextCycleFrom` (always `null` in M26 — owned by M30, present so the contract does not change), `trialEndsAt`, `nextDueDate`, `pastDueSince`, `suspendsAt` (= `coalesce(grace_until, past_due_since + 14 days)`, `PAST_DUE` only), `cancelAt`, `purgeAt` (only `SUSPENDED`; 90/180 days by `has_ever_paid`, `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Context 7 — returned as the **earliest** clean-up date, never a promise: no job acts on it in M26, so the web (S27) labels it "a partir de" / "earliest", not as a scheduled deletion), `planConfirmationRequired` / `planConfirmationFallbackAt` (`TRIALING` with `plan_confirmed_at` null; fallback = `trial_ends_at − 3 days`), `canDiscard` (**always `false` in M26** — M27), `addressRequired` (the full billing address is not saved), `items[]` (feature, tier, `unitPriceMinor` for display — items store no price, so each is read from a price version: for `ACTIVE` / `PAST_DUE` / `SUSPENDED` the items running now at the version in force today; **for `TRIALING` the plan that will be invoiced first** — the items scheduled for the first paid period (the default paid items starting at `trialEndsAt`, or the manager's confirmed selection), at the version in force on that period's start date, which is what S05 will charge; a trial's items are never "active now", so reading `startsAt <= now` would return an empty list), `totalMinor` (net of tax; BR has none), `openCharge` (the open `PERIOD` charge, or while `SUSPENDED` the open `RECOVERY` charge, with `hasPaymentLink` / `paymentLinkExpiresAt`; never a URL), `autoBilling` (`NONE`/`INACTIVE`, label null — the "Em breve" tab), `paymentConfirmationPending` (true while `payment_return_seen_at` is set and the charge is still open), and `upcomingPriceChanges[]` (dated list-price changes affecting the tenant's items whose version has `effective_from` in the future, `announced_at IS NOT NULL` and `superseded_at IS NULL` — a version the operator created but whose fan-out has not run, or a superseded one, is never shown). `GET /v1/billing/charges?limit=&offset=` returns the paginated history (Pattern A, `(tenant_id, due_date DESC)`): `id, kind, periodStart|null, periodEnd|null, dueDate, subtotalMinor, taxMinor (0), amountMinor, currency, status, method, paidAt, receiptUrl|null, lines[], taxLines ([])`. The summary **bypasses the BFF access cache** (UC-117) and an `exempt` tenant gets `exempt: true` with no actions. A staff or customer token → `403`. The BFF mapper returns **enums and flags, not display strings** (the BFF has no i18n: `apps/bff/src` imports nothing from `@ikaro/i18n`, and CLAUDE.md §7 puts visible copy in the web's `pt-BR` / `en` locale files); the web (S27) translates the status labels and the "Conta isenta de cobrança" notice from `status` / `exempt`. The mapper only shapes the response (flattens `openCharge`, drops internal fields, never emits a URL).

**Operator list extension (UC-111; owner of the work M25 promised).** M25-S15b's read-only `GET /internal/billing/tenants?status=` (`internal-billing-operator.controller.ts`) returns only subscription-derived fields in M25; this story, which already owns the Charge repository reads, adds its Charge-derived fields: **overdue age** (days since the oldest `OVERDUE` open charge's `due_date`, in the subscription's timezone), **last charge** (latest non-`CANCELED` charge: kind, status, amount, due date) and **open recovery** (the open `RECOVERY` charge, if any). The extension reads charges through the S02 repository in one batched, `tenant_id`-scoped query per page (no per-row query, no N+1), keeps the list offset-paginated and an investigation lookup (never a worklist), and changes no operator write.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`GetBillingSummaryUseCase`**, 2. **`ListBillingChargesUseCase`**, 3. **the existing M25 operator tenants-list use case** (modified to add the Charge-derived fields above).
**Backend HTTP surface:** new controller `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` — `GET /billing/summary`, `GET /billing/charges` (tenant from `RequestContext`; exact prefix mirrors the nearest sibling, e.g. `TenantSettingsController`, confirmed at story discovery).
**BFF endpoint spec:** extends `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts` — `GET /v1/billing/summary`, `GET /v1/billing/charges?limit=&offset=` (`@Roles('MANAGER')`, `@NoFeatureGate()`, `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)`); response types in `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts`. The backend routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` too (Wave 2 note).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none (no new error code is thrown here; labels are web locale keys added by S27).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/get-billing-summary.use-case.ts`, `list-billing-charges.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-billing-operator.controller.ts` (+ spec, integration spec) and the M25 operator tenants-list use case under `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/` (modify — exact file name as created by M25-S15b; Charge-derived fields)
- `apps/backend/http/billing/billing.http` (new)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, `billing.schemas.ts`, `billing.types.ts`, `billing.mapper.ts` (+ specs) (modify)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.component.spec.ts` (new)
- `apps/bff/http/billing/billing.http` (new)
- `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` (modify), `packages/types/src/index.ts` (modify only if a new export is needed)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager sees the status, dates, active features with prices, total, open charge, announced price changes and the full history; a trial, active, past-due, suspended (with the recovery charge and clean-up date), exempt and cancelling tenant each render correctly.
- [ ] A staff member or a customer cannot read any of it.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Summary per status (table-driven): `suspendsAt` with and without `grace_until`, `purgeAt` for never-paid (90) vs paid (180), `planConfirmationRequired`/fallback date, `canDiscard` false, `paymentConfirmationPending`
  - [ ] An item price read from the version in force today (a future version is only in `upcomingPriceChanges`)
  - [ ] Mapper: `exempt` flag, enum status, never a URL in `openCharge`; `timezone`, `nextCycle` / `nextCycleFrom` (null) present
  - [ ] A `TRIALING` summary lists the first-paid-period items priced at the version in force on that period's start; `upcomingPriceChanges` excludes a version with `announced_at` null or `superseded_at` set
- Integration:
  - [ ] Both backend and BFF routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (decorator spec; a suspended manager reaches them)
  - [ ] Pagination order/limit/offset and `total`/`hasMore`; the query uses the `(tenant_id, due_date DESC)` index (`EXPLAIN`)
  - [ ] Summary of a suspended tenant returns the open `RECOVERY` charge, not the stale period charge
  - [ ] `STAFF` and `CUSTOMER` tokens → `403`; reachable for a `SUSPENDED` tenant's manager
- Tenant isolation: [ ] tenant A's manager never receives tenant B's charges or subscription (explicit cross-tenant integration test)
- Operator list (UC-111): [ ] `GET /internal/billing/tenants?status=` returns overdue age, last charge and open recovery for a `PAST_DUE` and a `SUSPENDED` tenant, and `null`s for a tenant with no charge; one batched charge query per page (no N+1); `InternalApiGuard` + `PlatformAdminGuard` unchanged
- E2E: none — covered by integration; the page E2E is S35
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S15 — Payment link and payment return (UC-119)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-119 (all, A1–A5), UC-116 (the on-demand refresh), UC-117 A3, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (the two charge routes), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.charges` (`payment_return_seen_at`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (planned) and § Transactions
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge), M26-S07 (`RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase`), M26-S14 (BFF controller and types)
**Pattern:** Command use cases; provider I/O **outside** the transaction (M26-D3); reuse of the shared refresh (M26-D1).

**Description:**
Let the manager pay. `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-link` → `200 { paymentUrl, expiresAt }`: loads the tenant's charge; if it is not open (`PENDING`, `OVERDUE`, or the `RECOVERY` charge while suspended) → `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE` (default recommendation `409`; not payable = `CANCELED`, `REFUNDED`, `CHARGEBACK`, `FAILED`; `PAID` is not an error (A4); final status and set confirmed at story discovery); an already-paid charge returns the paid state so the page refreshes (A4). When the stored link is expired or missing the use case asks `BillingProviderPort.regeneratePaymentLink` (a port method already built by M25-S14 and listed in `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Provider port, M26-D10 — no port change here) (A1: Asaas's `invoiceUrl` has no documented expiry — the adapter re-reads the charge and returns the current URL; behaviour after the due date is re-verified by S37). **Locking (no lost update):** `charges` has no `version` column (`docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.charges`), so the use case reads the charge, calls the provider with no lock held (M26-D3), then opens a short transaction that takes the subscription row lock (`findByTenantIdForUpdate`, B-R1, M26-D2), **reloads the charge**, and writes only the link fields (`payment_url`, `payment_url_expires_at`) — a webhook that marked the charge `PAID` in between is never overwritten (the reloaded status wins; the stored link is still kept). A charge that has `provider_creation_status = PENDING` and no provider id (a provider outage, not an address hold) has no link to regenerate: the route answers `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` (default `503`, retryable; S05's retry creates it) without a provider call. `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-return` is called by the web when the manager returns from the hosted page: it records `payment_return_seen_at` (which lengthens the S06 settlement margin for a boleto that has not compensated) — written under the same B-R1 lock + charge reload as above, set once, idempotent — and calls `RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase` (S07) for an on-demand single-charge refresh; the response is the current charge state `{ status, paymentConfirmationPending }` and **never fails** because the provider is unreachable (the page keeps polling, A2). A `RECOVERY` charge created by the status sweep and still held for a missing billing identity or address (the one hold on a recovery charge — S06 / S05; a manager's own reactivate never creates one, S20) has no link to give: the route answers `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` (default `422`; **this story is its first thrower and owns the code**, reused by S19 and S20) without a provider call and the page sends the manager to "Dados de cobrança". A payment that fails leaves the charge open and the page offers "Tentar pagar novamente" (A3 — no server state).

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`GetOrRegenerateChargePaymentLinkUseCase`**, 2. **`RecordPaymentReturnUseCase`** (record, then refresh).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `billing.controller.ts` — `POST /billing/charges/:chargeId/payment-link`, `POST /billing/charges/:chargeId/payment-return`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** extends the BFF billing controller — `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-link` and `…/payment-return` (`@Roles('MANAGER')`, `@NoFeatureGate()`, `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)`; `chargeId` through `CanonicalParseUUIDPipe`); request: none; responses as above. The backend routes carry the same decorators.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** error codes first thrown here: `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE`, `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`, `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` — each added to `BillingErrorCode` and to both `errors.json` files (M26 rule, Wave 2 note).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/get-or-regenerate-charge-payment-link.use-case.ts`, `record-payment-return.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, `billing.schemas.ts`, `billing.types.ts`, `billing.mapper.ts` (+ specs), `apps/bff/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/charge.aggregate.ts` (+ spec) (modify — S02 already has `recordPaymentReturn(now)`; add the link-renewal behaviour, e.g. `renewPaymentLink(paymentUrl, expiresAt)`, which S02 does not have)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/http/billing-error.mapper.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE`, `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`, `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `error-mapper-coverage`)
- `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` (modify — the three `BillingErrorCode` members), `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/errors.json` and `packages/i18n/locales/en/errors.json` (modify — the three entries), `apps/web/shared/lib/i18n/error-codes-exhaustiveness.spec.ts` (modify only if the spec needs it)
- `apps/bff/src/shared/http/problem-detail.ts` (modify — `BffThrowableCode` only for a code the BFF itself throws; none expected here)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] The manager gets a working pay link for any open charge (including an expired one and the recovery charge), pays, returns, and sees "pagamento recebido, reativando…" until the payment lands.
- [ ] A charge that cannot be paid any more says so; one that is already paid just refreshes to paid.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each of A1–A5; `payment_return_seen_at` set once and idempotent
  - [ ] The refresh failing (provider unreachable) still returns the unchanged state without an error
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end on the fake: expired link regenerated (fake `LINK_EXPIRED`), return recorded, refresh applies a paid fact through `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` and reactivates a `PAST_DUE` tenant
  - [ ] The provider calls (link regeneration, the on-demand refresh) are outside any transaction (`transactional-io` passes)
  - [ ] A sweep-held recovery charge (incomplete address) → `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`, no provider call; a `PENDING`-creation charge with no provider id (not held) → `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, no provider call
  - [ ] **Race:** a webhook marking the charge `PAID` between the provider call and the link store is not overwritten (status stays `PAID`, link stored); the same for `payment-return` racing a webhook
  - [ ] Backend and BFF routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (decorator spec)
  - [ ] Another tenant's charge id → not found; a `STAFF` token → `403`
- Tenant isolation: [ ] a manager can only generate links for and mark returns on their own tenant's charges
- E2E: none — covered by integration; the pay flow E2E is S35
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S16 — Billing identity read and save (UC-118)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-118 (all, A1–A6), UC-113 (A8, A9 holds), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (identity), `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: BillingAccount, `docs/VALUE_OBJECTS_REFERENCE.md` (`Address`, `AddressSpec`, `CountrySpec`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Adding a new error — checklist
**Dependencies:** M25-S06 (`TaxId` VO, `CountrySpec` tax-ID entry), M25-S08 (`BillingAccount`), M26-S14 (BFF controller and types)
**Pattern:** Command use case on the `BillingAccount` aggregate; shared `Address` / `TaxId` VOs; validation by `CountrySpec`.

**Description:**
`GET /v1/billing/identity` and `PUT /v1/billing/identity` (every `PUT` field optional): `{ taxIdType: 'CNPJ'|'CPF'|'EIN'|'OTHER'|null, taxId, legalName, billingEmail, address: { street, number, complement?, neighborhood?, city, state, zipCode } }` → `200` with the same shape. **BR: the tax id is always present from signup, editable but never blankable** (the provider needs it); **US**: optional, no CNPJ / CPF type offered (A3; US is not offered in M26 but the validation is country-driven, not hard-coded). Validation by the billing country's `CountrySpec` through the shared VOs: an invalid tax id for the billing country (BR: bad CPF / CNPJ check digits; US: an `EIN` that is not 9 digits, a declared `OTHER` over 32 characters) → `422 BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID` (A1; this story adds the code to `BillingErrorCode` and both locale files, and the use case catches the VO's `TaxIdValidationError`; the billing-page copy and the UI hint must say alphanumeric CNPJs are accepted — `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/dev-notes.md` still says "14 dígitos"), invalid email → `422 BILLING_IDENTITY_EMAIL_INVALID` (A2), wrong postal code or state format → the existing `Address` error codes (A5). **Partial-PUT semantics:** every field is optional, but the stored `address` is an `Address | null` (`docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: BillingAccount) and `Address.create` requires street, number, city, state and zipCode (`apps/backend/src/shared/value-objects/address.ts`), so a stored address is complete by construction. A `PUT` that sends `address` **merges** the sent sub-fields over the stored address (or over nothing) and validates the merged whole through `Address.create(…, countrySpec.address)`; if the merge is still incomplete the call fails with the existing `Address` field errors and nothing is saved (a half-typed address is never stored). "Address missing" is therefore simply `address == null`. The full address is required **before the first paid plan selection** (NFS-e): until saved, `summary.addressRequired` is true (S14) and `PUT /plan` is rejected `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` (S19). The identity is **synced to the provider customer the next time a charge is issued** (S05) — saving never calls the provider. Saving an identity is also what lets a held subscription be issued automatically (UC-113 A8/A9): no re-trigger is needed because S05's selector sees the subscription due. It never blocks *using* the product. The form's labels / placeholders / fields come from `CountrySpec` on the web (S29); the API only validates.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`GetBillingIdentityUseCase`**, 2. **`UpdateBillingIdentityUseCase`** (partial update; BR tax-id blanking rejected).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `billing.controller.ts` — `GET /billing/identity`, `PUT /billing/identity`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** extends the BFF billing controller — `GET /v1/billing/identity`, `PUT /v1/billing/identity` (`@Roles('MANAGER')`, `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)`); the Zod body schema lives in `billing.schemas.ts` and **mirrors the backend validation by construction** (CLAUDE.md §8: grep both layers for duplicate schemas).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** **`BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID` and `BILLING_IDENTITY_EMAIL_INVALID` are first thrown here and are added by this story** (M25-S06 deferred the former: the `TaxId` VO throws `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_*`, and this story's use case catches `TaxIdValidationError` and throws its own `BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID`) (`BillingErrorCode`, both `errors.json`, mapper). Tax id, legal name and billing email are PII/financial data: never written to logs, span attributes or domain events by this story.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/get-billing-identity.use-case.ts`, `update-billing-identity.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/billing-account.aggregate.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `updateIdentity` with the merge-then-validate rule; "address missing" is `address == null`, no separate completeness method)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/http/billing-error.mapper.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — M25-S06 created it; map the identity errors, including the shared `Email` VO error and the `Address` error, `error-mapper-coverage` / `shared-vo-error-mapper-coverage`)
- `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` (modify — `BILLING_IDENTITY_EMAIL_INVALID`), `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/errors.json` and `packages/i18n/locales/en/errors.json` (modify), `apps/web/shared/lib/i18n/error-codes-exhaustiveness.spec.ts` (only if needed)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, `billing.schemas.ts`, `billing.types.ts`, `billing.mapper.ts` (+ specs), `apps/bff/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager can see and edit the billing identity with country-correct validation; a BR tenant can never blank its CPF/CNPJ.
- [ ] Saving a complete identity releases a held charge without any operator step.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each of A1–A6; BR blanking rejected; partial update leaves untouched fields; US optional tax id
  - [ ] Partial `address` PUT: merged over the stored address and validated whole for BR and US field sets; an incomplete merge stores nothing and returns the `Address` field errors
  - [ ] No tax id / legal name / billing email in any log line or event payload (spy on the logger in the integration test)
  - [ ] Backend and BFF routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (decorator spec)
- Integration:
  - [ ] Save → read round trip; invalid values return the documented codes and statuses (422) through the error mapper
  - [ ] **Hold release:** a subscription held by missing address (S05 fake) is issued on the next job run after the address is saved
  - [ ] `STAFF`/`CUSTOMER` → `403`; reachable while `SUSPENDED`
- Tenant isolation: [ ] a manager reads and writes only their tenant's billing account
- E2E: none — covered by integration; the tab E2E is in S35
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S17 — Plan reads — catalog and preview (UC-136)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-136 (main flow 1–2, A1–A6, A11), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (plan routes), `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: Subscription (item dependency graph, next-period scheduling), `docs/TENANTS_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.md` (chatbot tier source), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.subscription_items`
**Dependencies:** M25-S04 (`FeaturePrice`, selectable prices), M25-S08 (`Subscription` dependency graph and item scheduling), M26-S02 (the charge repository, for the next-period-paid fact), M26-S14 (BFF controller and types)
**Pattern:** Query use cases over the `Subscription` aggregate's pure methods (`validateSelection`, `previewSelection`); no persistence in this story.

**Description:**
The read side of the Plano tab. `GET /v1/billing/plan` returns the catalog for the tenant's country (every selectable feature with its tier prices, dependency hints — Loyalty needs Booking; chatbot tiers Básico / Plus), the **current** selection, the **scheduled next-period** selection (when one exists) and the effective dates ("entra em dd/mm" / "sai em dd/mm"); features without a list price for the country/tier are shown as unavailable (A6). `POST /v1/billing/plan/preview` takes the proposed `{ items: [{ featureKey, tier }] }` and returns each change with its effective date (the next paid service-period start) and that period's new total, or the specific rejection — dependency unmet / dependents active (`BILLING_FEATURE_DEPENDENCY_UNMET`, `BILLING_FEATURE_HAS_DEPENDENTS`, A1), empty selection (`BILLING_PLAN_EMPTY`, A5) — **without saving anything**. Read-only states are reported, not enforced here: `PAST_DUE` / `SUSPENDED` (A2) and a scheduled cancel (A4) set `readOnly` with the reason so the tab can explain; trial (A3) reports that all features stay active through the trial and the selection only determines the first paid period. The BOOKING-removal impact (A11) is **not** computed by preview (it needs S18); the removal guard is checked **at save only** (S19), whose `BILLING_FEATURE_IN_USE` response names what blocks it (UC-136 A11), while the UI shows a generic note beforehand — consistent with S30 and prototype `08i`. This keeps the removal check out of the preview. **Effective dates account for an already-paid next invoice (UC-136 A7 / A12):** the aggregate does not know about charges, so each use case loads the tenant's next-period `PERIOD` charge (`ChargeRepository`) and passes one fact, `nextPeriodPaid: boolean`, into `previewSelection`; when true, every change's effective date is the period start **after** the paid one and the response says so (`deferredBecauseNextInvoicePaid: true`). Save (S19) uses the same fact, so preview and save never disagree. **`exempt`** subscriptions are `readOnly` with reason `EXEMPT` (UC-117 A7: no plan editing) and a save is rejected `BILLING_PLAN_CHANGE_NOT_ALLOWED` (S19). **Response shapes (defined here, mirrored in `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` and written into `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API, which today describes these routes only in prose):** `GET /plan` → `{ countryCode, currency, readOnly: null | { reason: 'PAST_DUE' | 'SUSPENDED' | 'CANCEL_SCHEDULED' | 'EXEMPT' }, trial: { active: boolean, planConfirmed: boolean }, features: [{ featureKey, requires: FeatureKey[], tiers: [{ tier, unitPriceMinor | null, available: boolean, upcoming?: { amountMinor, effectiveFrom } }] }], current: [{ featureKey, tier, endsAt: date | null }], scheduled: [{ featureKey, tier | null, startsAt: date }] | null, totalMinor }` (`upcoming` per tier = an announced future price version, so the Plano tab can show the price-change note); `POST /plan/preview` → `{ changes: [{ featureKey, kind: 'ADD' | 'REMOVE' | 'TIER', fromTier | null, toTier | null, effectiveFrom: date }], newTotalMinor, effectiveFrom, deferredBecauseNextInvoicePaid }` or the specific rejection (`422`-class problem with the code). `PUT /plan` response is defined in S19.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`GetPlanCatalogUseCase`**, 2. **`PreviewPlanSelectionUseCase`**.
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `billing.controller.ts` — `GET /billing/plan`, `POST /billing/plan/preview`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** extends the BFF billing controller — `GET /v1/billing/plan`, `POST /v1/billing/plan/preview` (`@Roles('MANAGER')`, `@NoFeatureGate()`, `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)`); preview body `{ items: [{ featureKey, tier }] }` (unique `featureKey`s; a duplicate is a `400` from the Zod schema). Backend routes carry the same decorators.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** error codes first thrown here (raised by the aggregate's `validateSelection` / `previewSelection` from M25-S08 and surfaced by this story's routes): `BILLING_FEATURE_DEPENDENCY_UNMET`, `BILLING_FEATURE_HAS_DEPENDENTS`, `BILLING_PLAN_EMPTY` (default `422` each; confirm at story discovery) — this story adds them to `BillingErrorCode` and to both `errors.json` files (unless M25-S08 already did; grep first).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/get-plan-catalog.use-case.ts`, `preview-plan-selection.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/subscription.aggregate.ts` (+ spec) (modify — M25-S08 already names `validateSelection` and `previewSelection` (S17 consumes both, S19 consumes `validateSelection`); modify to add the `nextPeriodPaid` input to `previewSelection` and a read accessor for each feature's required features, for the dependency hints)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, `billing.schemas.ts`, `billing.types.ts`, `billing.mapper.ts` (+ specs), `apps/bff/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/http/billing-error.mapper.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — `BILLING_FEATURE_DEPENDENCY_UNMET`, `…_HAS_DEPENDENTS`, `…_PLAN_EMPTY`)
- `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` (modify — the three members), `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/errors.json` and `packages/i18n/locales/en/errors.json` (modify), `apps/web/shared/lib/i18n/error-codes-exhaustiveness.spec.ts` (only if needed)
- `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (modify — § Billing — Manager API: the `GET /plan` and `POST /plan/preview` response shapes above; S19 adds `PUT /plan`)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager sees every feature with its price, what they have, what is scheduled, when each change takes effect, and a live total; an impossible selection is explained, never silently accepted.
- [ ] A newly selected feature is shown as starting at the next paid period ("começa em dd/mm"), never as instant.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Preview for add / remove / tier change with effective dates (UTC-3 and DST tenants); dependency and dependents rejections; empty selection; unavailable price
  - [ ] Read-only flags for `PAST_DUE`, `SUSPENDED`, scheduled cancel and `exempt`; trial reports first-paid-period semantics
  - [ ] `nextPeriodPaid = true` moves every effective date to the following period start and sets `deferredBecauseNextInvoicePaid` (UC-136 A7)
  - [ ] Backend and BFF routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (decorator spec)
- Integration:
  - [ ] Catalog and preview through the HTTP layer; preview persists nothing (row counts unchanged)
  - [ ] `STAFF`/`CUSTOMER` → `403`
- Tenant isolation: [ ] the current and scheduled selections returned are the caller tenant's
- E2E: none — covered by integration
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean
