**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A visitor with a Google account can create a tenant with a business name, slug, country and CPF/CNPJ after accepting the Terms, and receives the first-manager invitation email; no JWT is issued and the dashboard is not entered by signup.
- [ ] A second signup by the same Google account is allowed only if every earlier self-signup tenant has paid, and a signup whose CPF/CNPJ already belongs to a never-paid, unpurged account is refused; otherwise the visitor is told exactly why and offered the sign-in link (and, until M27, the "contact Ikaro" message instead of discard).
- [ ] A taken, reserved or invalid slug, a failed Turnstile, an invalid tax id, unaccepted Terms and a rate limit each produce their own clear error and create nothing — and the visitor can **correct and retry immediately** without losing their trial.
- [ ] With `FEATURE_FLAG_SIGNUP_ENABLED` off, signup returns `503 SIGNUP_CLOSED` while slug availability and prices keep working.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each of A1–A10 mapped to its code/status (`400`, `409`, `422`, `429` with `Retry-After`, `503`); email/name never read from the body
  - [ ] Cookie: signed with `JwtService` and a `signup-session` `typ`/`aud` claim (a session JWT or OAuth state is rejected by the signup reader and vice versa), httpOnly, `Secure` outside local, `SameSite=Lax`, 30-minute expiry, only the three fields, cleared on `201`; tampered cookie rejected; an unverified Google email refused
  - [ ] Throttle override emits `429 SIGNUP_RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After` and is exercisable under `APP_ENV=local`
- Integration:
  - [ ] **Retry after failure:** after each of A1 (slug race), A2 (Turnstile), A5 (terms), A9 (tax id) and a provisioning failure, an immediate corrected retry succeeds (the `RESERVED` row was released); release is idempotent and refuses a `COMPLETED` row
  - [ ] Read-only eligibility inserts nothing and takes no lock; `existingTenantSlug` is the earliest unpaid tenant's slug; the same CPF/CNPJ from a second Google account is refused while the first account is unpaid and unpurged, and allowed once it has paid
  - [ ] The 4th signup reservation of one Google account in 24 h is refused with `SIGNUP_RATE_LIMITED`
  - [ ] **Concurrency (B-A1):** two simultaneous signups for one Google account produce exactly one reserved trial; the loser gets `SIGNUP_TRIAL_NOT_AVAILABLE`
  - [ ] End to end through the in-memory bus: signup → `TenantProvisioned` → trial subscription (M25-S11) and a first-manager invitation, with the registration completed and the tax id cleared
  - [ ] Two simultaneous signups for one slug → one tenant, the other `SIGNUP_SLUG_TAKEN`; a tenant-creation failure leaves no registration-completed row and no tenant
  - [ ] Turnstile via the fake verifier; the per-IP and per-Google throttles trip
  - [ ] No tenant-scoped `RequestContext` is read on any signup path
- Tenant isolation: [ ] a signup creates a tenant and never touches an existing tenant's data; the registration is bound to the new tenant only
- E2E: none — covered by integration; the page E2E is S35
- [ ] Checkov / Tfsec clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S25 — Signup resend (UC-127 A10)

**Agent:** `bff-ts + backend-ts`
**Complexity:** S
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-127 (A10), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Public Signup API (resend), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Staff OAuth login URL format and § Adding a new notification type, `journeys/guest/ikaro-trial.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S24 (the signup flow and the resend credential it issues at the `201`)
**Pattern:** A Staff-owned use case re-raising the existing `StaffInvited` event (the existing `StaffInvitedHandler` / Notification path sends the email); BFF orchestration — no new Platform → Staff call; a rate-limited public mutation authenticated by a signed credential.

**Description:**
`POST /v1/public/signup/resend` re-sends the first-manager invitation / login email from the account-created page. **Why Staff owns it:** no resend exists today. `InviteStaffUseCase` needs `firstName`/`lastName` and `Staff.reinvite()` throws on an empty name and overwrites name, role and `invitedBy`, while the first manager is created by `Staff.inviteFromProvisioning` with `name = null` (apps/backend/src/contexts/staff/domain/staff.aggregate.ts), and the signup cookie holding the Google name is gone after the `201`. So a new Staff `ResendFirstManagerInvitationUseCase` finds the pending MANAGER by `tenantId` + `email`, requires `googleOAuthId` to be null, **never touches name or role**, and re-raises `StaffInvited` (a new `Staff.resendInvitation(correlationId)` method that only adds the event). No new template; the existing email is sent.

**Identity of the caller (no enumeration):** the BFF reads the **resend credential cookie** issued at the signup `201` (S24: signed, `typ`/`aud` `signup-resend`, bound to `{ tenantId, email }`, 30 minutes). Without a valid credential the route answers one generic `401`; there is no body parameter naming a tenant or email, so the route cannot be used to probe whether an email belongs to a tenant. **Response:** `200 { sent: boolean, ready: boolean }` — `ready` is true when the first-manager row exists (so the account-created page can show the one-click Google sign-in to the existing staff login, S33), `sent` is true when an email was re-raised; if the row does not exist yet the answer is `{ sent: false, ready: false }` (the page keeps waiting); if the manager already linked their Google account the answer is `{ sent: false, ready: true }` and no email is sent. **Rate limits:** 3 per hour per tenant and 10 per day per IP (the existing in-memory BFF throttler, keyed on the credential's tenant id and on the IP — best-effort per instance, stated; the credential's 30-minute life bounds the abuse); over the limit → `429 SIGNUP_RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After`. When `FEATURE_FLAG_SIGNUP_ENABLED` is off the route answers `503 SIGNUP_CLOSED`.

**Backend use case steps:** `ResendFirstManagerInvitationUseCase` (Staff): load by `tenantId` + `email`, return `{ sent, ready }`, save inside `ITransactionManager.run()` so the outbox publishes `StaffInvited` atomically.
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `apps/backend/src/contexts/staff/infrastructure/controllers/internal-staff.controller.ts` — `POST /internal/staff/resend-first-manager-invitation` `{ tenantId, email }` (`InternalApiGuard`; `@NoFeatureGate()` and the explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)` the M25 detector requires). This replaces the `POST /internal/signup/resend` placeholder in `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (listed under **Notes for other owners**).
**BFF endpoint spec:** `POST /v1/public/signup/resend` — `@Public()`, credential cookie, throttles above; response above.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none (`SIGNUP_RATE_LIMITED` / `SIGNUP_CLOSED` are added by S24).

**Notes for other owners:** `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` — the resend internal route is `POST /internal/staff/resend-first-manager-invitation` (it replaces API_CONTRACTS' placeholder `POST /internal/signup/resend`; the docs editor made that replacement in the audit fix), `POST /v1/public/signup/resend` returns `{ sent, ready }` and needs the resend credential cookie; `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-127 A10 wording ("rate-limited resend" → credential-based, `ready` signal).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/staff/domain/staff.aggregate.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `resendInvitation(correlationId)`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/staff/application/use-cases/resend-first-manager-invitation.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/staff/infrastructure/controllers/internal-staff.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/staff/internal-staff.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/staff/infrastructure/http/staff-error.mapper.ts` (+ spec) (modify only if the use case adds a domain error)
- `apps/bff/src/features/platform/signup.public.controller.ts`, `apps/bff/src/features/platform/signup.public.schemas.ts`, `apps/bff/src/features/platform/signup-session.service.ts` (+ specs), `apps/bff/http/platform/signup.http` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A visitor who did not receive the email can ask for it again a limited number of times; the endpoint never reveals whether an email belongs to a tenant; the account-created page learns when the first manager exists.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit: [ ] rate limit trips; a missing or tampered credential gets the one generic `401`; the use case never changes name or role and refuses a manager whose Google account is already linked (`sent: false`); `ready` false when the row is absent
- Integration: [ ] end to end through the in-memory bus: resend produces exactly one invitation email; a second within the limit window is refused
- Tenant isolation: [ ] the resend can only target the tenant named by the caller's credential (issued by its own signup); a credential for tenant A never re-sends tenant B's invitation
- E2E: none — covered by integration
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S26 — `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` contract and owner-tenant restriction (UC-131)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-131 (all), `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 3 (module registry), § 4 `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` (the layout rules table is in § 4 "Validation: where each layer stops"), § 7 Adding a New Module (the checklist this story follows to the letter), § 12 (template catalogue), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Tenant Hotsite Manifest and the hotsite admin routes, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Schema-level enforcement and § `no-restricted-syntax` selectors, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` § Billing configuration (planned) (`PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG`)
**Dependencies:** M25-S07 (`PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG` in the env contract), M25-S25 (module filtering by entitlement, whose map this type must stay out of)
**Pattern:** Registry extension (the module-type union is closed and mirrored in several packages) + a domain layout rule; the same mirror-consistency discipline as every previous module.

**Description:**
Add the 13th hotsite module type, `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` — a call to action, **not a billable feature** (no price, no entitlement). Data contract (mirrors `BOOKING_CTA`'s vocabulary): `variant` (`centered` | `left-aligned`), `title` (1–80), optional `subtitle` (≤ 200), `ctaLabel` (1–30); **the link target is fixed to `/ikaro-trial` and is not a field**. The section background is **not** a data field: it is the section-level `presentation.surface` every module uses; whether the type accepts `presentation.background` (the image slot) is decided in `hotsite-layout.rules.ts`'s `PRESENTATION_MODULE_TYPES`. **Touch points (the registry is closed and mirrored; the mirror-consistency detectors enforce them):** `packages/types/src/enums.ts` (`HotsiteModuleType`), `packages/validation/src/hotsite.ts` (`HOTSITE_MODULE_TYPES` and the discriminated data schema) and `hotsite-modules.ts`, `packages/types/src/hotsite.ts` (data type), the contract test `packages/types/src/testing/hotsite-module-contract.ts`, the BFF `hotsite-admin.schemas.ts` module union (via `HotsiteModuleSchema`), and the backend layout rules. **Owner restriction:** `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/domain/hotsite-layout.rules.ts` rejects the type for any tenant other than the platform owner (slug equals `PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG`). The flag travels use case → aggregate → rule: `UpdateHotsiteContentUseCase` already holds `tenantRepo`, so it loads the calling tenant's slug, compares it with `PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG` (read through the config port the other Platform use cases use) and passes `isPlatformOwner` in the existing `LayoutValidationContext` to `HotsiteConfig.updateContent(…, ctx)` → `validateLayout` → `assertValidLayoutStructure(layout, { isPlatformOwner })`; the other caller, `hotsite-template-materializer.service.ts` (line ~68), calls `assertValidLayoutStructure(safeLayout)` **directly** (not `updateContent`), so this story changes that direct call and the function's signature (`assertValidLayoutStructure(layout, { isPlatformOwner })`, with the context type `LayoutValidationContext` in `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/domain/hotsite-config.types.ts`, currently only `maxBookingAdvanceDays`) — the materializer passes `false`; the aggregate's `validateLayout` (hotsite-config.aggregate.ts:257) passes the real flag. **Error (decided by the editor, simplest consistent with the existing hotsite errors):** a new `HotsiteModuleOwnerOnlyError` with code `PLATFORM_HOTSITE_MODULE_OWNER_ONLY`, field `layout`, mapped `422` by `platform-error.mapper.ts` as a single-cause error (`code` + `field`, not `violations[]`); confirm the status at story discovery. **Templates never carry it:** the template catalogue's test `packages/hotsite-templates/src/catalogue.spec.ts` ("lists all twelve module types exactly once") compares each template's layout to the full registry, so adding a 13th type breaks it for all six templates unless the test excludes this type explicitly (the test is reworded and the exclusion is a named constant `OWNER_ONLY_MODULE_TYPES`); applying a template that somehow carries it is **rejected** (the materializer passes `isPlatformOwner = false`, so the same error). The manifest includes the module when enabled; module filtering by entitlement (M25-S25, whose map is sparse: only `LEAD_FORM` and `CHATBOT`) already treats it as **not feature-gated** — a test pins it.

**How the editor learns the tenant is the owner (this story owns it):** `GET /tenants/hotsite` (backend `GetHotsiteContentUseCase`; BFF `GET /v1/tenants/hotsite`) gains `isPlatformOwner: boolean` — the same comparison as above, never a slug compared on the client — added to `HotsiteAdminContentResponse` in `@ikaro/types` (the `PATCH` response carries it too, since both return that type). **No staged rollout** (`docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 7: a member missing from either side fails `pnpm architecture-check`, and the web has exhaustive switches), so this story also does the **minimal web registration** to keep `tsc` and the existing suites green, and S34 adds the real component, panel and preview: `apps/web/features/platform/hotsite/module-schemas.ts` (register `TrialSignupCtaModuleDataSchema` in `MODULE_DATA_SCHEMAS`; the parity spec `module-contract.parity.spec.ts` needs it because an unregistered type validates anything), `page-model.ts` (`MODULE_SCHEMAS` entry), `apps/web/shells/hotsite/components/HotsiteModuleView.tsx` (a `case 'TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA'` returning `null` until S34 — the switch is exhaustive), its spec's `EXPECTED` record, the `hotsite.layout.modules.TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` label in both `web.json` files, and `default-layout.ts` / `manifest-schema.ts`: `MODULE_ORDER` stays the 12 shared types and a separate `OWNER_ONLY_MODULE_TYPES` set is added; `materializeLayout(existing, { isPlatformOwner })` appends the 13th disabled row **only for the owner** and `manifest-schema` accepts the type (and the owner-sized layout length) only when the draft belongs to the owner — otherwise a non-owner's Layout list would gain a row the backend rejects. S34's panel registry and visual editor consume the same flag.

**Backend use case steps:** `UpdateHotsiteContentUseCase` computes `isPlatformOwner` and runs the layout rule; `GetHotsiteContentUseCase` returns `isPlatformOwner`. **How it obtains the flag (decided):** today it only holds `HotsiteContentReader`, which carries no slug; it additionally injects `ITenantRepository` (the same port `UpdateHotsiteContentUseCase` uses) to read the calling tenant's slug and the same platform-owner config port (`PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG`; if the backend env entry does not exist yet, this story adds it to the env schema and contract) and compares the two — one comparison, shared with the update path.
**Backend HTTP surface:** reuses `PATCH /tenants/hotsite` (validation) and `GET /tenants/hotsite` (adds one response field) — no new route.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — `GET` / `PATCH /v1/tenants/hotsite` pass the typed response through.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n: the non-owner error entry in both `errors.json` files and the module label in both `web.json` files. No migration (`layout` is JSON; existing hotsites are unaffected), env var or flag.

**Notes for other owners:** `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 4 (owner mechanism = `isPlatformOwner` on the admin GET; the component is `TrialSignupCtaModule.tsx`), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (the field on `GET /v1/tenants/hotsite`; `PLATFORM_HOTSITE_MODULE_OWNER_ONLY` `422`), `docs/ERROR_CATALOG.md` (the new code `PLATFORM_HOTSITE_MODULE_OWNER_ONLY`, `422` — entry added in the audit fix; confirm status at discovery), `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-131.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `packages/types/src/enums.ts`, `packages/types/src/hotsite.ts`, `packages/types/src/testing/hotsite-module-contract.ts` (modify)
- `packages/validation/src/hotsite.ts`, `packages/validation/src/hotsite-modules.ts` (+ specs) (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/domain/hotsite-config.types.ts` (modify — `LayoutValidationContext` at :296 gains `isPlatformOwner`), `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/domain/hotsite-layout.rules.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — owner rule and the new `assertValidLayoutStructure` signature, `PRESENTATION_MODULE_TYPES` entry), `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/domain/hotsite-config.aggregate.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `LayoutValidationContext` gains `isPlatformOwner`; `validateLayout` passes it), `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/domain/errors/platform-domain.error.ts` (modify — `HotsiteModuleOwnerOnlyError`), `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/application/services/hotsite-template-materializer.service.ts` (+ spec) (modify — passes `false`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/application/use-cases/get-hotsite-content.use-case.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `isPlatformOwner`)
- `packages/types/src/hotsite-schemas.ts` (modify — the strict module schema location per `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 7), `packages/types/src/hotsite.ts` (`TrialSignupCtaModuleData`, `isPlatformOwner` on `HotsiteAdminContentResponse`)
- web minimal registration (see the Description): `apps/web/features/platform/hotsite/module-schemas.ts`, `page-model.ts`, `default-layout.ts`, `manifest-schema.ts`, `apps/web/features/platform/hotsite/module-contract.parity.spec.ts` (if it needs the new cases), `apps/web/shells/hotsite/components/HotsiteModuleView.tsx` + `HotsiteModuleView.spec.tsx`, `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/LayoutTab.tsx` (+ spec, passes the owner flag to `materializeLayout`), `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (+ specs for each logic file)
- the non-owner error code plumbing (`PLATFORM_HOTSITE_MODULE_OWNER_ONLY`, M25-S06 pattern): `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts`, `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/errors.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/errors.json`, `apps/web/shared/lib/i18n/error-codes-exhaustiveness.spec.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/infrastructure/http/platform-error.mapper.ts` (+ spec) (modify — shared with S24, so S24 and S26 are **not** batchable)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/platform/application/use-cases/update-hotsite-content.use-case.ts` (+ spec) (modify — pass `isPlatformOwner` into the layout rule)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] The platform-owner tenant's manager can save a layout containing the `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` module; any other tenant's attempt is rejected; no template includes it and no existing hotsite changes.

## Wave 4 — Frontend

> **Shared frontend rules for every Wave 4 story** (cited once): the web slice is `apps/web/features/billing/{api,components,hooks,model}/` (created by the first story that needs it, S27); routes live under `apps/web/app/dashboard/billing/` and `app/[slug]/…`, kept thin (CLAUDE.md §7 Testing); server data uses `bffServerFetch` (authenticated, server-only) and client mutations / polling use `bffClient` through React Query hooks (precedent: `apps/web/features/booking/api/availability-alerts.server.ts` / `availability-alerts.ts`); `useTenant()` is the only source of `tenantId` in hooks; **never a raw `fetch()`** and never `--ba-*` in dashboard components (Tailwind + shadcn only); `ls apps/web/shared/components/ui/` and reuse what exists (`section-card`, `badge`, `button`, `alert-dialog`, `select`, `switch-field`, `pill-select`, `card`) before writing a control; every new component ships its `.spec.tsx` in the same commit; every visible string uses `useTranslations()` with keys in **both** `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json` and `…/en/web.json` (and every backend error code the UI maps is added with its translations by the backend story that first throws it (M25-S06 covers only `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_REQUIRED`, `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_INVALID` and `BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID`; see decision M6)); Playwright specs are test cases only, reusable flows live in `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/`. **Cross-cutting Wave 4 rules (decisions R6, R9):** (1) one shared summary query key `['billing','summary',tenantId]` (tenant from `useTenant()`), used by the Resumo, the tabs and the S32 banner; **every billing mutation** (pay, identity save, plan save/confirm, cancel, reactivate) invalidates it (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § A mutation that changes server state owned by another feature must invalidate that feature's query keys), each with a spy-based unit test. (2) **Money and dates:** every `*Minor` amount is converted to major units before `formatMoney(amount, locale, currency)`, with `currency = summary.currency` (never the tenant's display currency); date-only values (`dueDate`, `nextDueDate`, `effectiveFrom`, `YYYY-MM-DD`) are parsed with `utcDateFromISO` (`shared/lib/formatting/calendar-day.ts`), never `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')`; instants (`trialEndsAt`, `suspendsAt`, `cancelAt`, `purgeAt`) are formatted in the **subscription timezone** (`summary.timezone`, S14), not the settings timezone. (3) **Retention copy (R6):** no screen promises erasure or warning emails before M27 — use "Seus dados são mantidos por pelo menos {90|180} dias; depois dessa data (a partir de {data}) podem ser apagados", with `purgeAt` always labelled as the earliest date; where no `purgeAt` exists (cancel pages) the generic wording "pelo menos 90 dias (180 se já houve pagamento)" is used. (4) **Form feedback:** saves report through `ActionPane`, `FeedbackMessage`, `FieldFeedback` and `shared/lib/feedback/use-form-feedback` (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § A form's error or success message lives in the action pane); public forms use `shared/components/hotsite/branded-feedback-message`; never a hand-written `role="alert"`. (5) Error messages come from `resolveApiErrorFeedback` on the error `code`, never from `detail`. The Wave 4 stories share `packages/i18n/locales/*/web.json` and the billing page files and are therefore **sequential in practice** (see `## Run-batch preview`).

### M26-S27 — Billing page shell, Resumo and Cobranças tabs (UC-117)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-117 (all, A1–A8), `docs/DASHBOARD_FRONTEND_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Staff/Manager Shell (the planned Assinatura entry), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (summary and charge history), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Authoring new i18n UI copy keys, `docs/BFF_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Web → BFF Transport Layer, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S14 (summary and charges reads), M25-S29 (the route-to-feature registry the nav entry uses, and the `/dashboard/suspended` manager notice page this page replaces — M25-S31a only adds the staff variant)
**Pattern:** Server Component fetch (`bffServerFetch`) hydrating the shared summary query (`['billing','summary',tenantId]`) with client sub-components for tabs; one shared `generateMetadata()` fetch via React `cache()`; tabs as a `?tab=` query parameter (`resumo|plano|cobrancas|dados|automatica`, the journey's contract, also the deep-link target of S30/S32) on a `role="tablist"` bar written in the billing slice following the `ServiceEditTabBar` precedent (no cross-slice import; `ui/` has no tabs primitive).

**Description:**
Build the manager's "Assinatura" page (`/dashboard/billing`): one page, five tabs and one primary action per tab — **Resumo**, **Plano** (S30), **Cobranças**, **Dados de cobrança** (S29) and **Cobrança automática** (shown as "Em breve" until M29; no endpoint). Destructive actions (cancel, S31) are separate confirmation pages. This story builds the shell, the nav entry and the two read tabs. **Resumo** shows the status, trial end or next due date, the active features with tier and price, the total, the open charge with the **Pagar agora** call to action (wired in S28), the automatic-billing status, announced price changes, the cancel link and "Falar com a gente"; every state in `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/` (01–01o) is covered: loading skeleton and load error with retry (A1), trial, active, past-due, suspended (reason, the open recovery charge and the earliest possible clean-up date — labelled as such, not a scheduled deletion, since no job acts on it before M27; A5), suspended-canceled, suspended-operator, payment-reversed, no-charges (A2), price-change notice, **exempt** ("Conta isenta de cobrança" on every tab, no actions — A7) and the trial "Confirmar plano" prompt with the automatic-invoice date (A8); a staff or customer opening the route gets the 403 / redirect (A6). **Cobranças** is the paginated history (subtotal, total, status, method, receipt link on paid charges; BR has no tax lines). The "Pagamento recebido, reativando…" polling state (A3) is S28's. The nav entry "Assinatura" is **manager-only** (Sidebar / BottomNav / MoreSheet, `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/`) and is registered in the M25-S29 route-to-feature registry as a deliberately ungated route (the backend counterpart is `@NoFeatureGate`) so it stays reachable while suspended; its label is the new key `dashboard.nav.billing`, and the entry lives in `Sidebar.tsx` and `MoreSheet.tsx` (`BottomNav.tsx` only needs the drill-down hide for `/dashboard/billing/cancel`, no new item). **Shell wiring (every dashboard section needs it):** a new `app/dashboard/billing/layout.tsx` calling `loadDashboardShellContext` and rendering `DashboardSectionShell` (without it the page has no Locale/Tenant providers); `/dashboard/billing` added to `MANAGER_ONLY_ROUTES` in `proxy.ts` (+ `proxy.spec.ts`) so STAFF is redirected to `/dashboard` (A6 — `proxy.ts` is modified for this one line only; the suspended redirect is never in `proxy.ts`); `shells/dashboard/model/topbar-route.ts` `PAGE_TITLE_KEYS` gains the billing title and the cancel page's back link. The Plano and Dados tabs render a neutral "em breve" placeholder until S30 and S29 replace them (Wave 4 is sequential). The loading skeleton is written with the repo's inline `animate-pulse` blocks (no skeleton primitive exists in `ui/`). **Resumo also covers** the scheduled-cancel state `06b` (ACTIVE with `cancelAt`: grey alert "Cancelada — acesso até dd/mm", badge "Cancelando", cancel link hidden; the "Reativar assinatura" action is mounted by S31), the interim discard line when `canDiscard` is false ("Para descartar este teste, fale com a gente"), and the retention line per the Wave 4 copy rule. **"Falar com a gente"** links to the owner tenant's hotsite booking page: `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/booking`, with the slug read server-side from the web env introduced by S33 (S27 renders the link from a shared helper `getTalkToUsHref()` that S33 backs with the env; until S33 lands it falls back to hiding the link). Money, dates and the shared summary key follow the Wave 4 rules above.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/01-billing-active.html`, `01b`–`01o`, `01q` (annual is M30 — not built), `journeys/manager/billing.md`.

**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S14.

### M26-S28 — "Pagar agora" — payment link, return and polling (UC-119)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-119 and UC-117 (A3, A4), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (payment-link, payment-return), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S15 (the two endpoints), M26-S27 (the page and the Resumo tab)
**Pattern:** React Query mutation + a bounded polling query keyed on `paymentConfirmationPending`.

**Description:**
Wire the Resumo's primary action. **Pagar agora** calls `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-link` and navigates to the provider's hosted page; the `returnUrl` passed to `createCharge` at issue time (S05; Asaas `callback`, usable only if Asaas's callback-domain rule accepts the web host — verified in S37) brings the manager back to `/dashboard/billing?payment=return` (the marker is part of the `returnUrl` S05 passes and S15 documents; if the provider cannot honour a return URL, the click also stores `sessionStorage['billing.payReturn']` so a manual return to the page is recognised; a later visit with `paymentConfirmationPending` already true resumes polling without a new return call). **Return detection:** the page treats `?payment=return` (or the sessionStorage marker) as the return, calls `POST …/payment-return` once for the charge in the marker, removes the marker, then shows "pagamento recebido, reativando…" (`paymentConfirmationPending`), **polling `GET /v1/billing/summary` every 5 seconds for about a minute** (A3) until the status leaves pending or the window ends (then a calm "ainda processando" message with a manual refresh). Link expired / failed payment → a regenerate / retry affordance (A4: "Gerar novo link"); payment in processing (A2: Pix / boleto compensation) → "pagamento em processamento"; charge already paid → the page refreshes to the paid state; `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE` → an explanation and a summary refresh. **Failure (`01o`, decided by editor):** the API gives no failure signal (payment-return answers only `{ status, paymentConfirmationPending }`), so `01o` is **not a distinct state**: a return whose charge is still open after the polling window shows the calm "ainda processando" message with "Tentar pagar novamente" (`payment-link` again) beside the manual refresh. **Other outcomes of S15's contract:** `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` on `payment-link` → `FeedbackMessage` above the open-charge card ("Não foi possível abrir a página de pagamento") with the button still enabled (`journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/dev-notes.md` "Specified but not drawn", row "Payment-link failure"); `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` (a sweep-held recovery charge, decision R1) → the manager is sent to the Dados tab; `openCharge.hasPaymentLink = false` (charge not yet created at the provider) → the button is disabled with "estamos preparando o link" and the summary is refetched, never a payment-link call. States match `01i`, `01j`, `01c`, `01d`. After every pay/return/regenerate call the shared summary key is invalidated. While `SUSPENDED` the action pays the **recovery** charge. The polling stops when the page is hidden (visibility API) and never runs for exempt tenants.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/01i-billing-payment-pending.html`, `01j-billing-payment-link-expired.html`, `01c-billing-past-due.html`, `01d-billing-suspended.html` (`01o-billing-payment-failed.html` is folded into the "ainda processando" state — see the Description; the prototype needs a one-line note, listed for the journeys owner).
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S15's two routes and the summary.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S15.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.pay.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/PayNowButton.tsx`, `PaymentPendingBanner.tsx`, `PaymentLinkExpiredNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/utils/payment-return-marker.ts` (+ spec) (new — reads/clears `?payment=return` and the sessionStorage marker)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/usePayCharge.ts`, `usePaymentConfirmationPolling.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingSummaryTab.tsx` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager can pay any open charge (period or recovery) on the provider's page, returns, sees the pending state and then the paid state without reloading; an expired link is regenerated; a failed payment can be retried.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Polling: 5 s cadence, stops after the window, stops when hidden, stops on a paid status (fake timers)
  - [ ] Each of A2–A5 renders its state; the return call is made exactly once and only when the marker is present; no marker and `paymentConfirmationPending` false → no call
  - [ ] `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` and `hasPaymentLink = false` each render their state; the summary key is invalidated after each call
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-pay-flow.spec.ts` (fake provider: create the link, simulate `PAID_PIX`, assert the page leaves the pending state)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S29 — "Dados de cobrança" tab (UC-118)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-118 (all), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (identity), `docs/VALUE_OBJECTS_REFERENCE.md` (`CountrySpec`, `Address`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S16 (identity endpoints), M26-S27 (the page and tabs)
**Pattern:** Country-driven form rendered from `CountrySpec`; reuse of the CEP lookup hook; React Hook Form + Zod mirroring the BFF schema.

**Description:**
The identity tab. The form is **rendered from the billing country's `CountrySpec`** — labels, placeholders, formats and fields follow the country in the tenant's language (BR: Logradouro, Número, Complemento, Bairro, Cidade, UF, CEP with ViaCEP autofill through the existing `useAddressLookup` hook in `features/booking/hooks/` — reused, not duplicated; US: Street, Number, Apt/Suite, City, State, ZIP Code, no neighbourhood). Fields: tax-id type (`CNPJ` / `CPF` / `EIN` / `OTHER`) and number (**BR: always present, editable, never blankable**; US optional, with `EIN` or `OTHER` offered as the type (the user declares it) — A3), legal name, billing email, address. Client validation mirrors the BFF schema; server errors map to fields (`BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID`, `BILLING_IDENTITY_EMAIL_INVALID`, postal/state format — A1, A2, A5); save failure keeps the form (A4); success shows the saved state (`02d`) in the action pane. Save, field errors and the form-level error go through `ActionPane` / `useFormFeedback` / `FieldFeedback` (the first invalid field is focused); a successful save invalidates the shared summary key so `addressRequired`, the S32 banner and the Plano tab update without a reload. Between the Resumo and this tab, a loading skeleton and a tab-level `FeedbackMessage` with retry cover a failed identity read. While the full address is missing (`summary.addressRequired`), the tab shows the required-address state (`02e` / `02f`) and a note that it unlocks the first paid plan selection. The tab is read/write even while suspended.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/02-billing-identity-br.html`, `02b-billing-identity-br-erro.html`, `02c-billing-identity-us.html`, `02d-billing-identity-saved.html`, `02e-billing-identity-us-address-required.html`, `02f-billing-identity-br-address-required.html`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S16's `GET`/`PUT /v1/billing/identity`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S16.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.identity.*` in both locales.

- `apps/web/features/billing/model/identity-form.ts` (+ spec) (new — Zod schema and `CountrySpec` field mapping)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)

### M26-S30 — "Plano" tab and "Confirmar plano" (UC-136)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-136 (all, A1–A13), UC-130, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (plan routes), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S17 (catalog and preview), M26-S19 (save and confirm), M26-S21 (the chatbot tier mapping — the Plus tier must be real before it is sold), M26-S27 (the page)
**Pattern:** Controlled form with a debounced preview query; one primary action whose label depends on the trial state; saves report through `ActionPane` / `useFormFeedback` and invalidate the shared summary key (`planConfirmationRequired`, the S32 banner).

**Description:**
The plan tab lists every feature with its price for the tenant's country, a toggle, the chatbot tier choice (Básico / Plus), dependency hints and a **live total**; each change is previewed with its effective date ("entra em dd/mm" / "sai em dd/mm") through `POST /v1/billing/plan/preview` (debounced on every change). One primary action: **Salvar** (`PUT /v1/billing/plan`), which during the trial is labelled **Confirmar plano** (and the untouched default is confirmed through `POST /v1/billing/plan/confirm`, A13 → "já confirmado"). States match the prototype: edit (`08`), trial choose (`08b`), dependency error (`08c`), saved (`08d`), read-only while suspended (`08e`) or past due (`08f`) or cancelling (A4), scheduled next-period selection (`08g` — "Alterações agendadas para dd/mm", current and scheduled selections side by side, replaceable until the next invoice is paid; there is no first-billed lock), save error (`08h`), BOOKING in use (`08i` — names what blocks the removal, A11), plan-confirm required (`09`). A newly selected feature is described as starting at the next paid period ("começa em dd/mm"), never as instant (the locked-feature screens say the same); a paid next invoice defers the change to the following renewal and says so (A7, A12); an unavailable price shows the feature as unavailable (A6); the missing-address rejection links to the Dados tab (A10). `BILLING_FEATURE_*` errors surface their specific messages and the selection is kept (A9). `BILLING_FEATURE_IN_USE` names what blocks the removal from the response `params` S18/S19 define (counts per category — bookings, recurring schedules, availability alerts — and the effective date), rendered as `08i` does. A dated list-price change on a feature (`upcoming` per item in `GET /v1/billing/plan`, S17) shows the read-only note "Novo valor a partir de dd/mm: R$ …" (`01k`). The locked-feature pages from M25 ("Contratar este recurso") open `/dashboard/billing?tab=plano`; this story wires that link in `LockedFeaturePage.tsx`. Prices use the Wave 4 money rule.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/08-plan-edit.html`, `08b`–`08i`, `09-plan-confirm-required.html`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S17 and S19.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S17 and S19.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.plan.*` in both locales.

### M26-S31 — Cancel and reactivate pages (UC-133, UC-134)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-133 and UC-134 (all), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (cancel, reactivate), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S20 (cancel / reactivate endpoints), M26-S27 (the page)
**Pattern:** Destructive actions on their own confirmation page (`alert-dialog` for the second confirm where the prototype uses one); status-driven wording.

**Description:**
Cancel is a **separate confirmation page** reached from the Resumo's "Cancelar assinatura" link: the wording depends on the status — `ACTIVE`: access continues to dd/mm, nothing refunded (`06`); `TRIALING` with the first invoice paid: access continues to the end of the paid period (`cancelAt`, decision R5; `06e`); `PAST_DUE` / unpaid trial: immediate suspension with the explicit note that nothing was paid (`06d`, A3). The page cannot know before the click whether a trial's first invoice is paid (the summary carries no such flag), so the trial wording is decided from `summary.openCharge` (open first charge, or none issued yet → the immediate wording) and a paid first invoice is reported by the response `cancelAt`; where the two disagree the outcome screen (`06b` or `06d`) is authoritative and the confirm copy for a `TRIALING` tenant reads "se a primeira fatura já foi paga, o acesso continua até o fim do período pago; senão a conta é suspensa agora" (decided by editor — no new summary field). **Data paragraph (decision R6):** "Seus dados são mantidos por pelo menos 90 dias (180 se já houve pagamento); depois disso podem ser apagados. Você pode reativar pagando 1 mês." — never "apagados definitivamente" or "avisos 30 e 7 dias" (no purge job or warning email exists before M27); the prototype copy of `06`/`06d`/`06e` is superseded. Outcomes: scheduled (`06b` "Cancelada — acesso até dd/mm") with an "Desfazer cancelamento" action; immediate (`06d`); already cancelling (`409` → shows the scheduled state, A1); error with retry (`06c`, A4). **Reactivate**: "Reativar assinatura" for a scheduled cancel or a `CANCELED` suspension; for a payment-based suspension the Resumo already shows "Pague 1 mês para reativar" with the recovery charge's **Pagar agora** (S28). `POST /v1/billing/reactivate` returns the open charge; the page then calls `payment-link` (reactivate never returns a URL) — a provider failure shows `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` with retry (A3); a missing billing address answers `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` with nothing created (decision R1) and sends the manager to the Dados tab (A6). Cancel, undo and reactivate invalidate the shared summary key. An `OPERATOR` suspension shows no reactivate action (only the operator lifts it, `01n`). The annual wording (`13`) is M30.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/06-cancel-confirm.html`, `06b-cancel-scheduled.html`, `06c-cancel-error.html`, `06d-cancel-immediate.html`, `06e-cancel-trial-paid.html`, `01e-billing-suspended-canceled.html`, `01n-billing-suspended-operator.html`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S20.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S20.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.cancel.*` and `…reactivate.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/app/dashboard/billing/cancel/page.tsx` (new — thin)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/CancelSubscriptionPage.tsx`, `CancelOutcome.tsx`, `ReactivateAction.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingSummaryTab.tsx`, `BillingStatusBadge.tsx` (+ specs) (modify — mounts `ReactivateAction` and the scheduled-cancel state `06b`/badge "Cancelando"; the cancel link)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/feature-registry.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `/dashboard/billing/cancel` as an ungated route; M25-S29's spec asserts every dashboard page is mapped)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useCancelSubscription.ts`, `useReactivateSubscription.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager can cancel with wording that matches exactly what will happen (scheduled vs immediate, nothing refunded), undo a scheduled cancel, and reactivate a cancelled or payment-suspended subscription by paying one month; an operator suspension offers no reactivation.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Each prototype state and status-dependent wording; the already-cancelling and error paths; reactivate → payment-link call sequence; no reactivate action for `OPERATOR`; `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` on reactivate → Dados link
  - [ ] The cancel and suspended pages never contain "apagados definitivamente" or "30 e 7 dias" (named test); the summary key is invalidated by each mutation
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-cancel-reactivate.spec.ts`

### M26-S32 — `SubscriptionBanner` and the suspended-manager repoint (UC-130, UC-121)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-130 (all, A1–A6) and UC-121 (manager routing), `docs/DASHBOARD_FRONTEND_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Staff/Manager Shell (there is no single shared layout, but every section layout renders `shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx`, which is the mount point), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Tenant access enforcement (item 6 — the suspended redirect lives in the layouts via `loadDashboardShellContext`, never in `proxy.ts`), `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S14 (summary fields), M26-S27 (the billing page, the `?tab=` deep links and the shared summary key — S32 repoints to it), M26-S30 (the Plano tab the "Confirmar plano" link opens), M25-S29 (the shared suspended-redirect helper called from `loadDashboardShellContext` / the section layouts — the root `app/dashboard/layout.tsx` is a passthrough and does **not** hold it — plus the `/dashboard/suspended` page and the feature registry)
**Pattern:** One banner component mounted **once** in `shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx` — the single component all ten section layouts render, inside the Locale/Tenant providers — above `{children}`; it reads the shared summary query (`['billing','summary',tenantId]`, with a `staleTime` because it runs on every dashboard navigation); status-driven variants; fail-silent.

**Description:**
**The banner.** `SubscriptionBanner` (manager-only, mounted once above the page content, not dismissible) reads the summary: `TRIALING` → "Faltam N dias do seu teste" linking to billing, with "Confirme seu plano para gerarmos a primeira cobrança" and the **Confirmar plano** call to action until `plan_confirmed_at` is set (trial entitlements stay fully enabled); last days → "sua conta será suspensa se a primeira cobrança não for paga", "Seu teste termina hoje", singular "Falta 1 dia" (A1); `PAST_DUE` → the same component in the amber alert style: "Pagamento em atraso." with the amount, due date, `suspendsAt` and **Pagar agora**, access full and nothing locked, disappearing when the payment is confirmed (A3); billing address missing (BR or US) → an amber banner "Adicione seu endereço de cobrança — sem ele sua conta será suspensa em {fim do teste}" linking to the Dados tab (A4); `ACTIVE`, exempt or load error → no banner (A5, A6 — a load error fails **silent**: no banner, the page is never blocked). **Where it mounts** (decision R9): `DashboardShell.tsx`, the one component every section layout renders (`DashboardSectionShell`, `DashboardLayoutShell` and the hand-rolled bookings/loyalty/schedule layouts all end in it), so the banner appears on all ten sections with no per-layout edit; `SubscriptionBannerProvider` wraps it there and is exactly one instance. **Precedence:** one banner at a time, by priority `PAST_DUE` (pay) > billing address missing > plan unconfirmed trial > trial countdown (`SUSPENDED` shows no banner: the manager is on the billing page); `banner-state` is table-driven over the combinations. **"Pagar agora" in the banner is a link to `/dashboard/billing`** (it never calls `payment-link`). The summary call is fail-silent. **The repoint.** M25 sent a suspended manager to `/dashboard/suspended` (the notice); UC-121 sends them to the billing page. Change only the target of the M25 shared helper (`suspended-redirect.ts`, called from `loadDashboardShellContext`; `proxy.ts` is **not** modified — it cannot read tenant status, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Tenant access enforcement, item 6) so a `SUSPENDED` **manager** lands on `/dashboard/billing`, while the staff notice page stays for `STAFF`; the trial-ended-unpaid case lands with "Pague 1 mês para reativar". **Loop guard:** the helper also skips `/dashboard/billing` and every subpath (as it already skips `/dashboard/login` and `/dashboard/suspended`). **Disabled navigation (UC-117 A5, owned here):** while the tenant is `SUSPENDED` the manager's Sidebar, MoreSheet and BottomNav items other than "Assinatura" render `aria-disabled` with no `href`, driven by the suspended flag the shell already receives from the access answer (M25-S29). **`ManagerSuspendedNotice` (decided by editor):** it stays as the page `/dashboard/suspended` renders for a manager who opens that URL directly, but with a link to `/dashboard/billing`; no redirect points to it any more.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/04-dashboard-trial-banner.html`, `04b-dashboard-past-due-banner.html`, `01d-billing-suspended.html`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — reads `GET /v1/billing/summary` (S14).
**Backend HTTP surface:** none.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.banner.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/SubscriptionBanner.tsx`, `SubscriptionBannerProvider.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/model/banner-state.ts` (+ spec) (new — pure status → variant mapping incl. day counts in the tenant timezone)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — mounts `SubscriptionBannerProvider` and `SubscriptionBanner` above `{children}`; passes the suspended flag to the nav)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/Sidebar.tsx`, `MoreSheet.tsx`, `BottomNav.tsx` (+ specs) (modify — disabled items while suspended)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/suspended-redirect.ts` (+ its spec) (modify — the suspended manager's target changes from `/dashboard/suspended` to `/dashboard/billing`; skip `/dashboard/billing` and subpaths; the helper stays called from `loadDashboardShellContext`, never the root `app/dashboard/layout.tsx`)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/ManagerSuspendedNotice.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — link to the billing page)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/feature-registry.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A trial manager always sees how many days remain and, until confirmed, the call to confirm the plan; a past-due manager sees the amount, the dates and a pay button, and nothing is locked; an active or exempt manager sees nothing.
- [ ] A suspended manager is taken to the billing page and nowhere else; staff keep the notice.
- [ ] The banner never blocks a page, even if its data fails to load.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] `banner-state` table-driven (singular/plural, last day, tenant-timezone boundaries, address-missing, exempt); component fails silent on a load error
  - [ ] Suspended-redirect helper: suspended `MANAGER` → billing, `STAFF` → notice, `CUSTOMER` unchanged; **no redirect on `/dashboard/billing` and `/dashboard/billing/cancel`** (loop guard)
  - [ ] Banner on every one of the ten sections (a spec renders each layout's shell and finds exactly one banner); banner precedence combinations; the banner reuses the shared summary key; suspended manager's other nav items are `aria-disabled` without `href`
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-banner-and-suspension.spec.ts`
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S33 — `/ikaro-trial` public pages, Terms and Privacy hosting (UC-127, UC-128)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-127 (all, A1–A10) and UC-128, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Public Signup API (BFF, no JWT), `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` (the `/ikaro-trial` routing paragraph), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § CSP allowances for a new external UI resource and § Hotsite full-page components, `docs/CI_TRAPS.md` § Cloudflare Turnstile's test sitekey, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/guest/ikaro-trial.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S22 (slug availability), M26-S23 (prices), M26-S24 (session, signup, `termsVersion`, the dev signup-session issuer), M26-S25 (resend), M25-S30 (the `Unavailable` and hotsite shell conventions)
**Pattern:** Public pages branded as the owner tenant (`--ba-*` allowed: this is the hotsite tree), built under `app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/…` for the platform-owner slug only (any other slug gets `notFound()`), reached through the rewrite `/ikaro-trial/*` → `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/ikaro-trial/*` (`docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § The `/ikaro-trial` pages); independent of `isPublished`. The public URLs are `/ikaro-trial`, `/ikaro-trial/entrar`, `/ikaro-trial/conta-criada` (the `nextPath` S24 returns), `/ikaro-trial/termos`, `/ikaro-trial/privacidade` — **all under the one `ikaro-trial` segment**, so no new top-level segment appears under a tenant slug and the reserved list of S22 needs no new word. Every full-page component paints `backgroundColor: 'var(--ba-background)'` (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Hotsite full-page components).

**Description:**
The public self-signup flow, following `journeys/guest/prototypes/ikaro-trial/`: **details** with the BR price table (`02`; loading `02c`, error `02d` — signup stays possible, A1 of UC-128; the US variant `02b` is **not offered** where the country has no price list, i.e. in production); the **Google step** (`03`; **no Turnstile here** — decision R9: Turnstile is verified only on the final `POST /v1/public/signup/tenant`, so a token never has to survive the full-page Google redirect; the prototype's `03`/`03b` Turnstile parts are superseded, the `03b` "verification failed" screen becomes the failure state of the form step's widget; "trial already used" `03c` with "Entrar na conta existente" and — until M27 — the honest "fale com a gente" message instead of discard; the Google button is a link to the BFF signup start that S24 settles); the **form** (`04`, rendered on `/ikaro-trial/entrar` once `useSignupSession` returns a session: business name, slug with live availability check from S22, country (Brasil only), CPF/CNPJ for BR with check digits, a **fresh Turnstile widget** (the lead-form `TurnstileWidget`, token single-use, re-requested after every failed submit), the Terms/Privacy checkbox; states `04b` slug taken **with client-generated suggestions** (typed slug plus a suffix, each re-checked through S22's availability call — UC-127 A1), `04c` validation, `04d` submitting, `04e` rate limited with `Retry-After`, `04f` creation failed; and a "cadastro indisponível" notice when the BFF answers `SIGNUP_CLOSED`, decision R7); the **account-created / check-email** page (`05`) with the rate-limited "reenviar e-mail" and, once the first manager exists, a one-click Google sign-in to the existing staff login (A10). Email and name come from the session and are shown read-only; the language derives from the country (no language field). **Terms and Privacy hosting (decision R9):** the backend env `SIGNUP_TERMS_VERSION` is the single source of the version; `GET /v1/public/signup/session` returns `termsVersion`, the form shows the checkbox link to the hosted text for that version and sends the same value back as `acceptedTermsVersion`; **the web has no duplicate version constant**. The owner-supplied legal texts are hosted per version as static content (`features/billing/legal/terms-texts.ts` maps version → text; the pages `termos` and `privacidade` render the text for `?v=` or the current version); the story hosts them but **does not write them** (a gate: until the owner supplies the final texts the story ships clearly-marked placeholder pages, `noindex`, that block launch in S39). A spec asserts a hosted text exists for the version the API returns (the dev/CI `SIGNUP_TERMS_VERSION` value is read from the backend's `.env.example`). **Routing and CSP:** the `/ikaro-trial/*` → `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/ikaro-trial/*` rewrite is done **in `proxy.ts` at runtime** (a `next.config.ts` rewrite would be baked at build time, and the web container's env is runtime-only); `proxy.ts` sees the original `/ikaro-trial/…` path, which `isHotsiteRoute()` already treats as a hotsite route, so the whole tree **already gets the Turnstile CSP** — `ikaro-trial` must **not** be added to `RESERVED_TOP_SEGMENTS` (a spec asserts every route of the tree gets `challenges.cloudflare.com` in script/frame/connect-src, which also covers the soft navigation from the hotsite CTA). `x-pathname` keeps the original path, so `shared/lib/i18n/resolve-locale.ts` `STATIC_SEGMENTS` gains `ikaro-trial` (otherwise every request fires a manifest lookup for a slug named `ikaro-trial`). **Web env (devops half):** the web needs `PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG` as a server-only runtime env (read in `proxy.ts` for the rewrite and by `getTalkToUsHref()` for "Falar com a gente", S27): add it to `apps/web/.env.example`, to `WEB_REQUIRED_SERVER_ENV_KEYS` in `packages/infra-scripts/src/env-contract.ts`, and to the web `env_vars` in `infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf` and `envs/prod/main.tf`; the change is a plain non-secret env var, so it is one app+Terraform PR carrying the `infra-app-mix-ok` label (the playbook row for a plain env var, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook; confirm the row at story discovery). **Live-verification check:** `gcloud run services describe <web>` shows the variable; `curl -I https://<web>/ikaro-trial` returns the owner tenant's page (200, not 404) and the response CSP contains `challenges.cloudflare.com`; pass = all of it. **SEO:** `/ikaro-trial` is indexable (title/description via `generateMetadata`, canonical `/ikaro-trial` so `/{ownerSlug}/ikaro-trial` is not a duplicate); `/entrar`, `/conta-criada` and the placeholder legal pages return `robots: { index: false, follow: false }` (the lead-form precedent). **Transport:** the public prices fetch uses `bffPublicFetch` in `features/billing/api/public-prices.server.ts`; the session/slug/signup/resend calls are client hooks through `bffClient` on the same-origin `/v1` gateway (the signup cookie is httpOnly and set on the web origin by the gateway); the `Retry-After` header of a `429` is surfaced by extending `bffClient`'s error interceptor/`ApiError` with an optional `retryAfterSeconds` (the web has no Retry-After handling today), used by `04e` and the resend countdown. Copy lives under the existing `hotsite.*` namespace (`hotsite.ikaroTrial.*`), not a new top-level namespace; messages use `BrandedFeedbackMessage`/`BrandedFieldFeedback` (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § A form's error or success message… Public hotsite). The E2E Google step uses S24's dev/test signup-session issuer (decision R9), not the staff dev-login.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/guest/prototypes/ikaro-trial/01-ikaro-hotsite-cta.html`, `02`–`02d`, `03`–`03c`, `04`–`04g`, `05-welcome-trial-started.html`, `journeys/guest/ikaro-trial.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S22–S25.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `hotsite.ikaroTrial.*` in both locales; web server-only env `PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG` (devops half above, `infra-app-mix-ok`); no Terms version constant (the API returns it).
**IAM / permissions:** none.
**Live-verification check:** see the Description (web env var present, `/ikaro-trial` serves the owner page, CSP carries Turnstile).
**PR sequence:** 1 PR (plain env var, app + Terraform together, `infra-app-mix-ok`).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/entrar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/conta-criada/page.tsx` (owner slug only, `notFound()` otherwise) (new — thin; the rewrite mechanism is settled at story discovery)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/IkaroTrialDetails.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialPriceTable.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialGoogleStep.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialSignupForm.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/SlugField.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/AccountCreatedPage.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialAlreadyUsedNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useSignupSession.ts`, `useSlugAvailability.ts`, `useCreateTenantSignup.ts`, `useResendInvitation.ts` (+ specs), `apps/web/features/billing/model/slug-suggestions.ts` (+ spec) (new)
- `apps/web/features/platform/api/signup.ts` (+ spec) (new — client-only `bffClient` calls mirroring the BFF `platform` slice the signup routes live in, `docs/BFF_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Web → BFF Transport Layer; there is no server-side signup fetcher: the cookie-bound session read cannot use `bffServerFetch`/`bffPublicFetch`)
- `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/termos/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/privacidade/page.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/legal/terms-texts.ts` (+ spec) (new)
- `apps/web/proxy.ts` (+ `proxy.spec.ts`) (modify — the runtime rewrite `/ikaro-trial/*` → `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/ikaro-trial/*`; no CSP change, the tree is already a hotsite route)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/i18n/resolve-locale.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `ikaro-trial` in `STATIC_SEGMENTS`)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/api/bff-client.ts`, `errors.ts` (+ specs) (modify — `retryAfterSeconds` on `ApiError`)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/public-prices.server.ts` (+ spec) (new — `bffPublicFetch` of `GET /v1/public/billing/prices`)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/usePublicPrices.ts` (+ spec) (new — retry for `02d`)
- `apps/web/.env.example` (modify), `packages/infra-scripts/src/env-contract.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `WEB_REQUIRED_SERVER_ENV_KEYS`), `infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf`, `infra/terraform/envs/prod/main.tf` (modify — web `env_vars`)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A visitor can read the features and Brazilian prices, pass Turnstile, sign in with Google, choose a free slug, enter CPF/CNPJ, accept the Terms and create a trial account, then be told to check their email — with a clear, specific message for every failure (taken slug, invalid tax id, rate limit, trial already used, creation failure).
- [ ] The US option is not offered; the Terms and Privacy pages are reachable from the checkbox and their version is recorded.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Each prototype state renders; slug availability debounced; CPF/CNPJ check digits; form submits no email/name and sends back the API's `termsVersion`; a fresh Turnstile token is requested after each failed submit
  - [ ] `proxy.spec.ts`: every route of the `/ikaro-trial` tree gets the Turnstile CSP and is rewritten to the owner slug; `RESERVED_TOP_SEGMENTS` is unchanged; every full-page component paints `var(--ba-background)`
  - [ ] A hosted legal text exists for the `termsVersion` the API returns; `SIGNUP_CLOSED` renders the notice; `ApiError.retryAfterSeconds` is read from `Retry-After` and drives `04e`; slug suggestions are re-checked through the availability call; form errors use `BrandedFeedbackMessage`
  - [ ] `/entrar` and `/conta-criada` set `noindex`; `/ikaro-trial` sets the canonical
- E2E:

### M26-S34 — `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` component and editor panel (UC-131)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-131 (all), `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 3, § 4 `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` and § Adding a New Module, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Hotsite full-page components and § CSP allowances for a new external UI resource, `docs/DASHBOARD_FRONTEND_ARCHITECTURE.md`, `journeys/manager/hotsite.md` (the appended Billing section), `journeys/manager/prototypes/hotsite/dev-notes.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S26 (the module contract, the owner rule, `isPlatformOwner` on the hotsite admin GET and the minimal web registration), M26-S33 (the `/ikaro-trial` target exists)
**Pattern:** Registry-driven module addition mirroring `BOOKING_CTA` (public component + editor config panel + lazy registry + module schema), owner-only offering in the editor.

**Description:**
The public component and the editor panel for the 13th module. **Public:** a call to action that renders `title`, optional `subtitle`, a button with `ctaLabel` and the fixed target `/ikaro-trial` (the target is not configurable), with the `centered` / `left-aligned` variants (default label "Testar grátis por 14 dias"), rendered as a server component **through `HotsiteSectionShell`** (which owns the section surface, so the module does **not** paint `--ba-background` itself — that rule is for full-page views, and painting it would override `presentation.surface`), using `sectionTextColor` / `sectionCardBackground` and `var(--ba-*)` only (`docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 7. Adding a New Module, step 2); the link is a `next/link` to `/ikaro-trial`; disabled → not rendered, `/ikaro-trial` still works if linked (A3). **Editor:** a `TrialSignupCtaConfigPanel` in `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/modules/` (title, text, button label, variant, presentation), registered in the lazy-panel registry; **offered only to the platform-owner tenant's manager** — the editor reads `isPlatformOwner` from the hotsite admin `GET` response that S26 adds (decision R9), with the backend rule of S26 as the enforcement (A1) and the editor only a convenience. **Owner-aware registry (S26 registers the type minimally so `tsc` stays green; this story completes it):** `MODULE_ORDER`/`materializeLayout` (`features/platform/hotsite/default-layout.ts`) and `manifest-schema.ts` (`MODULE_TYPE_SET`, `tooManyModules`) take the owner flag and omit `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` for every other tenant, so a non-owner's layout list still shows twelve rows and its saves never carry the type (a spec per non-owner path); `DEFAULT_MODULE_DATA` gains an entry. **File discovery follows the §7 checklist** (every exhaustive `Record<HotsiteModuleType, …>` and switch), not a grep of `BOOKING_CTA`: that includes `LayoutTab.tsx`, `ModuleConfigPreviewScreen.tsx`, `hotsite-module-buffer.ts`, `hotsite-template-application.ts`, `HotsiteEditor.tsx` and the `hotsite.layout.modules.TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` label. Validation errors (empty label, title too long) show inline (A2). Section background is the section-level `presentation.surface`, not a data field. The editor/panel states are in the prototype's appended GAP screens (`01g`, `01h`). No CSP change is needed: `/ikaro-trial` is already a hotsite route with the Turnstile allowance (S33); this story adds a spec that the hotsite → `/ikaro-trial` soft navigation keeps Turnstile working.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/hotsite/01g-module-config-trial-signup-cta.html`, `01h-module-config-trial-signup-cta-erro.html`, `journeys/manager/hotsite.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — saves through the existing `PATCH /v1/tenants/hotsite`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none new (S26).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under the hotsite module copy in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/modules/TrialSignupCtaConfigPanel.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx`) (new)
- `apps/web/shells/hotsite/components/HotsiteModuleView.tsx` (modify — render the new type)
- `apps/web/shells/hotsite/components/TrialSignupCtaModule.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx`) (new — named `XxxModule.tsx` per the §7 checklist)
- `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/hotsite-editor-lazy-panels.tsx` (+ spec) (modify)
- `apps/web/features/platform/hotsite/module-schemas.ts`, `module-presentation.ts`, `render-plan.types.ts`, `page-model.ts`, `default-layout.ts`, `presentation-tokens.ts`, `manifest-schema.ts` (+ specs) (modify — owner-aware `MODULE_ORDER`/`materializeLayout`/`manifest-schema`; complete the entries S26 registered minimally)

## Wave 5 — Tests, operations and launch

### M26-S35 — Money-loop and suspended-experience E2E on the fake

**Agent:** `frontend-ts + backend-ts`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/TESTING_STRATEGY.md` § Layer 5 — End-to-End Tests (Playwright), § Running Playwright locally, § E2E Selector Strategy, § Testing the billing provider — fake, sandbox, contract suite and clock, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_TESTING.md` § An E2E test that computes "today" and § An integration test seeding fixtures under fixed tenant UUIDs, `docs/CI_TRAPS.md` § Cloudflare Turnstile's test sitekey, `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-138
**Dependencies:** M26-S13 (simulate / seed / clock), M26-S26 (the `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` contract and `isPlatformOwner`), M26-S28, M26-S29, M26-S30, M26-S31, M26-S32, M26-S33, M26-S34 (the pages under test — S29 for `billing-identity.spec.ts`, S34 for `hotsite-trial-signup-cta.spec.ts`), M25-S12 (the dev-clock reset it uses), M25-S31b (the suspended-experience spec it extends)
**Pattern:** Page-object / helper flows in `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/`; the spec files are test cases only; time and provider events are driven **only** through the dev endpoints, never by a test-only path inside a use case.

**Description:**
The spanning scenario the milestone promises, as Playwright specs against the local compose stack with the **fake provider** (`BRL_FAKE`): `ikaro-trial-signup.spec.ts` (S33's signup, from the Ikaro hotsite CTA to the account-created page and the first-manager invitation, Turnstile test sitekey); `billing-money-loop.spec.ts` (provision a trial tenant → trial banner → confirm the plan → advance the clock to ten days before trial end → the first charge exists → **Pagar agora** → simulate `PAID_PIX` → paid → advance past trial end → `ACTIVE`; a later charge unpaid → `PAST_DUE` banner → past 14 days → `SUSPENDED` with the recovery charge → pay → `ACTIVE`); `billing-page-states.spec.ts`, `billing-identity.spec.ts`, `billing-plan.spec.ts`, `billing-cancel-reactivate.spec.ts`, `billing-banner-and-suspension.spec.ts` and `hotsite-trial-signup-cta.spec.ts` (the specs S27–S34 each named); and the **manager half of the suspended experience** that M25-S31b could not test (the billing page as the only reachable page). "Today" in any date assertion is computed in the **tenant timezone** the same way the app does, never from the runner's clock. Fixtures that use fixed tenant UUIDs have symmetric, complete setup / teardown (the shared Postgres container is reused across unrelated CI runs). Local E2E runs only in the story's worktree via `pnpm infra:e2e` and `pnpm infra:e2e:down` (CLAUDE.md §0).

**Clock isolation (the dev clock is process-global and only moves forward).** `apps/web/playwright.config.ts` runs `fullyParallel: true` with `workers: undefined` **locally only** (CI already runs `workers: 1`, so the race is a local-run hazard; the dedicated project still protects local runs and any future CI worker increase), and a +40-day advance followed by a manual sweep could suspend any other non-exempt tenant created by a concurrently running spec. The clock-moving specs (`billing-money-loop`, `billing-banner-and-suspension`, `billing-cancel-reactivate`, `billing-plan`, and any other spec that calls `billing-clock.ts`) therefore run in a **dedicated Playwright project `billing-serial`** (`fullyParallel: false`, `workers: 1`, declared to run **after** the default project via `dependencies`; the existing project is named `chromium` (`apps/web/playwright.config.ts` :32), so `dependencies: ['chromium']` must name it exactly), each on tenants created for that spec only (never the shared seeded tenants). M25-S12 exposes a dev-clock **reset** (note for the M25 owner: `POST /internal/dev/clock/reset`, local/staging only); `billing-clock.ts` resets the offset in `afterAll`, and an AC asserts that no spec outside `billing-serial` observes a non-zero offset. Asynchronous effects of the cron publisher routes (`POST /cron/billing-…` only publishes the trigger) are awaited with `expect.poll` against the page or an API read, never `waitForTimeout`. S33's **soft-navigation spec** (the Turnstile widget loads when the visitor arrives from the hotsite CTA) is owned here as `ikaro-trial-soft-navigation.spec.ts`. The CI E2E job has a 30-minute cap with the suite already near 15 minutes (`.github/workflows/pr-tests.yml`, "shard it rather than raising the cap"): the story records the added wall-clock and, if the job exceeds 25 minutes, shards the `billing-serial` project into its own job instead of raising the cap. The signup specs obtain the signup-session cookie through the dev/test issuer S24 adds (APP_ENV-gated), not through Google.

**Backend use case steps:** none.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — uses the dev endpoints (S13, M25-S12).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/e2e/billing-money-loop.spec.ts`, `billing-page-states.spec.ts`, `billing-identity.spec.ts`, `billing-plan.spec.ts`, `billing-cancel-reactivate.spec.ts`, `billing-banner-and-suspension.spec.ts`, `billing-pay-flow.spec.ts`, `ikaro-trial-signup.spec.ts`, `ikaro-trial-soft-navigation.spec.ts`, `hotsite-trial-signup-cta.spec.ts` (new)
- `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/billing-seed.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/billing-clock.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/billing-simulate.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/billing-page.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/index.ts` (new)
- `apps/web/playwright.config.ts` (modify — the `billing-serial` project), `.github/workflows/pr-tests.yml` (modify only if the E2E job needs sharding, see the timing AC)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] The full path from a visitor's signup to a paid, active subscription — and from a missed payment to suspension and recovery — works end to end in a real browser on the local stack, with every actor-visible state asserted.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit: none — E2E story (helpers carry their own `.spec.ts` where logic exists)
- Integration: none
- Tenant isolation: [ ] the scenarios run with two tenants; tenant B never sees tenant A's billing page or charges
- E2E:
  - [ ] The ten specs above pass locally and in CI; no `waitForTimeout` (`expect.poll` for cron effects); selectors via the E2E selector strategy
  - [ ] **Clock isolation:** the clock-moving specs run only in the `billing-serial` project after the default project, on spec-owned tenants; the offset is reset after them and a check proves no other spec observes a non-zero offset
  - [ ] **Timing:** the CI Playwright job stays under its 30-minute cap with headroom (shard `billing-serial` into its own job if it exceeds 25 minutes)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code (helpers)
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean
